## 2026-07-28 — Release v1.23 (terminal-lit rule: every terminal lights its first tile at start)
- **Every path terminal now lights its first tile from the very first render** (user rule + screenshots: a dark terminal stub made the board read wrong; elbow endpoints can still point the wrong way — lit ≠ solved).
- **Singular root cause was a longstanding asymmetry**: rotateToExpose pins ALL paths' endpoint tiles at build end, but rerollAllRotations/rerollRotationsOnly skipped only PATH 1's `entry`/`exit` — the 2-path canonical loop's rerolls re-randomized secondary endpoints (the screenshot). Both passes now skip all endpoints via new `endpointCellKeys()`. Safe vs the canonical loop's entry/exit swaps (rerolls only run outside the swap, and the set carries all 8 refs anyway).
- **Quad mode**: turning a quad permutes sub-tile positions, so endpoint quads may only take turns that keep their notch stub(s) exposed. New `allowedQuadTurns(qr,qc,pqr,pqc)` checks by SIMULATION (4 rotateQuads = identity, grid untouched); twin pairs share one turn value so the qualifying set intersects both quads. Applied at all three turn sites: scrambleQuads (pool = qualifying∪{0}, never empty; non-endpoint quads unchanged uniform 0-3), ensureMinScramble (no qualifying turn → skip without consuming quota), breakPreWonPaths (no qualifying turn → re-pick another cell next guard iteration).
- Composes with v1.22's depth quota (endpoint tiles were already excluded from "twistable"). Test: fresh 2-path singular boards + quad boards — every terminal shows a lit stub at start; paths still scrambled beyond it.
- Version bumped 1.22 → 1.23.

## 2026-07-28 — Release v1.22 (minimum start-scramble guarantee per path)
- **Paths can no longer spawn nearly solved** (user screenshot: a pink path with ONE wrong tile — solution read-off-obvious): random rotation rerolls leave each path tile solved by luck (straights 50%, crosses always, elbows 25%), and breakPreWonPaths only caught the fully-won extreme. New `ensureMinScramble()` (maze.js) guarantees ≥`MIN_PATH_SCRAMBLE_FRACTION` (0.58 — user-tuned from 0.5 same session; at most ~42% of a path may spawn correct) of each path's TWISTABLE tiles start off their solved ports, floor 2 per path.
- Twistable excludes entry/exit cells (notch-pinned) + crosses (4-symmetric); "solved" is port-equivalence-aware via rotationsHaveSamePorts (straight at solution+2 counts as solved — matches what the player sees). Quad mode's unit is the QUAD: quota over quads carrying the path's cells, scrambled = non-zero quadScramble turns. Twins lockstep everywhere (mirrors breakPreWonPaths). Same documented trade-off: runs post-canonical-checks → marginally off-canonical boards accepted.
- All four newPuzzle branches funnel through new `finalizeStartScramble()` (ensureMinScramble → updateHighlighted → breakPreWonPaths). PITFALL caught in-session: maze.js `shuffle()` returns a COPY (doesn't mutate) — a bare `shuffle(arr);` statement silently keeps scan order (top-left bias).
- Rollout: worker/urgent/new-PotD-seed builds all covered (shared maze.js); already-seeded PotD snapshots unchanged. Test: fresh small multi-path boards — no path should start missing only 1-2 tiles.
- Version bumped 1.21 → 1.22.

## 2026-07-28 — Release v1.20 (first-time-player funnel, PotD 5-hint cap, faster break-fade)
- **⚠ DEPLOY ORDER: back-end FIRST.** New `first_run_stats` table (auto-created by db.create_all) + `POST /api/first-run/sync` + admin `GET /api/admin/first-run/stats`. Until the back-end is live, front-end syncs 404 — returning players self-heal via the dirty-flag retry, but a first-visit-only player's row is lost.
- **First-time-player funnel** (user's 6 questions): one row per NEW browser, keyed by persistent session_id, created ONLY when the auto-start run fires (pre-feature players never get rows — aggregates are cleanly post-deploy first-timers). Fields: initial_run_puzzles (count of auto-start-run solves, 0 for no-touch bails), howto_clicked (hooked in Tutorial.open — covers all 3 entry points), tutorial_completed (markWatched), potd_nudge_clicked (BOTH tooltip variants — in-run + menu pitch; same seen-key so mutually exclusive per player), daily_more_clicked (recorded in the hudQuit CLICK handler, not quitToMenu — the nudge action also routes through quitToMenu and must not count), outside_started/outside_solved (any non-first-run start/solve: menu Zen/Marathon, resumes, PotD — same sitting or later visits).
- Funnel delivery: localStorage state (`_firstRunStats_v1`) + whole-state sync with max/OR server merge (idempotent → dumb retry safe); dirty flag re-syncs on next load; raw fetch keepalive with NO 5s abort (cold-dyno rationale, same as late-milestones flush). All firstRun* Tracking calls no-op unless the state object exists.
- **PotD hint cap: 5/attempt** (`MAX_HINTS` in potd.js). Why now: ineligible retries tick hintsUsed AND count for Streak/Career — unlimited hints = brute-force streak farming. Cap applies to retries on purpose; Marathon (25% penalty) and Zen untouched. Gate lives in game.js handleHintClick (single choke point); HUD button reads "Hint n/5" during PotD play (numeric — zero new i18n keys), disables at 0 (joined the Undo :disabled CSS rule), restores plain label via showMenu + quitToLeaderboard (leaderboard exit skips showMenu). Resume restores the count; pre-cap saves with >5 resume at 0 remaining; replays unaffected. NO server-side >5 rejection — old cached PWA clients still submit legitimately. tooltip.potdHint gained "— max 5 per puzzle" ×15 langs; stale "will disqualify" fallback in potd.js fixed.
- **Broken-path fade 2× faster**: BROKEN_FADE_MS 1500 → 750 (render.js); 200ms flash unchanged, total 1.7s → 0.95s.
- Version bumped 1.19 → 1.20.

## 2026-07-28 — Release v1.18 (corner-hop paths blocked: pair distance floor + layered backtracker reserve)
- **Two ~5-cell corner-hop paths shipped on a 4-path singular board** (user screenshot): v1.16's pair-retry fix covered the LINEAR placement (2-3 paths) only — this was the backtracker, via a two-part hole. (1) `pickFarPair` is farthest-of-5 with NO lower bound; late in a 4-path build ~6 used perimeter cells kill enough samples that a distance-2 corner pair can win by default, and endpoints that close can ONLY yield hops. (2) The backtracker's quality gate opened entirely below 80 of 200 budget steps — deep searches routinely get there, then accept FIRST-FIT (the span check needs just 2 rows + 2 cols, so a corner curl passes).
- Fix 1: **distance floor in pickFarPair** — min Manhattan `max(4, (ROWS+COLS)/3)`; samples extend to 3× base when unmet; best-found always returned (never fails a build). Protects path 1 + linear + backtracker alike. Floor vs max-possible checked across sizes (5×7: 4/10; 12×12: 8/22) — prunes corners, can't starve.
- Fix 2: **layered backtracker reserve** (was binary): >80 steps = full q≥5 gate; 80–40 = length-only (`addedPathMinCells`, ~8-12 cells); <40 (`BT_LENGTH_RESERVE`) = accept-anything emergency lane. Never-drop-a-color guarantee intact; "plain path" can no longer mean "5 cells" until the search is on fumes.
- Caveat: screenshot board may have been a pre-v1.16 save resumed post-update, but the traced mechanism exists in current code regardless. Watch: 4-path build times (middle layer spends budget choosier) + all 4 colors still placing.
- Version bumped 1.17 → 1.18.

Always true; the new Zen moves line made it visible.

Hard refresh (or any second visit) resolves; nothing to fix.

## 2026-07-28 — Release v1.16 (CG-retrial pass: Zen feedback, first-run fast track, quad/gate retunes, path-pair fix)
- **Zen twists-vs-minimum feedback**: solve popup now shows "Twists: {m} · minimum: {b} = {p}% solve rate" (p = min/twists), or a gold Perfect line when m ≤ min (deliberately `<=`, no % — beating the floor via a shortcut shows Perfect, not a broken >100%). `Maze.minSolveMoves()` computes the floor at startRecording (port-equivalence-aware: cross=0, straight ≤1, elbow ≤2; quads via ext-port-shift equivalence, twins priced as one unit; `Gates.minMovesToClear`) and stamps `recording.minMoves` — survives save/resume; old saves just hide the line. Twist count = rotate/gate/undo + hints with turns>0 (undo counts on purpose; locks and 0-turn hints free). Zen-only display, Marathon untouched.
- **First-visit fast track** (`MARATHON.FIRST_RUN_TIERS: [3,2,2,2]`, null disables): auto-start run compresses tiers on the VARIETY axis only (growth accrues normally), then puzzle 10 hands off to QUAD at 1 path / start size — violet "New tile type" cue on the solve card (`solveQuadUp`, replaces tier-up at that boundary). New `levelConfig(lev)` in marathon.js is the per-level source of truth (quadMode varies BY LEVEL on the fast track) — replaced every decodeType+pathCountFor pair; upcomingDims carries per-level config so pre-gen crosses the mode flip. Fixed alongside: HUD label (letter+digit now from levelConfig, was type-string) and PotD nudge trigger (now fires at L5 on the compressed ladder). Hand-off cacheKey == 'q' starter key (verified) → instant reveal, no "Building puzzle…".
- **Quad explainer tooltip added then REMOVED same session (user call)**: players still conceptually twist ONE tile; describing the 4-sub-tile position shuffle explains implementation, not experience. Solve-card cue is announcement enough — don't re-add (comment left in onPuzzleReady). Tutorial still doesn't teach quad (rebuild = separate task if ever wanted).
- **Quad starts 3×3 logical** (6×6 physical; MIN_DIM_QUAD + _4PATH → 3) — both Zen and Marathon, all tiers; starter/hand-off/debug-toggle all derive.
- **Gates are now SIZE-based, not solve-based**: target = 1 + floor(growthUnits/4), growthUnits = physical (rows+cols) − mode start sum (singular 8, quad 12). Same rate per SUB-TILE: singular = old +1/4-solves exactly; quad = +1/2-solves; start-size quad board = 1 gate (fast-track hand-off included — solve-count formula would have opened it at 3). Zen additionally opens GATE-FREE for puzzles 1-2 (run-level solveCount<2; Marathon untouched — clock makes easy openings a scoring advantage). Known composition quirk: menu Quad Zen debuts gates at 2 (0,0,2,…) — the 1-gate stage falls inside the gate-free window. Gate count also dips with the tier-drop sawtooth now (same size = same gates) — accepted, size is the difficulty axis.
- **Border-hugging path fix** (user screenshot: 9-cell yellow path along row 0, 2 bends, ZERO interior, shipped on a 3-path board): the quality floors re-ranked ROUTES but all candidates shared ONE endpoint pair — pickFarPair's Manhattan metric is blind to border-pinning (W-row0→E-row1 scores 8 on an 8-wide grid), and when every route scores 0, betterPath1's length tie-break actively prefers the longest border run. Fix: `addPathWithPairRetry` — floors can now reject a PAIR (≤ MAX_PAIR_ATTEMPTS=3 pairs, early-out at score ≥6 of 7; crossing excluded from the bar, it's board-dependent); same loop wraps path 1 (early-out ≥4, winning pair re-pinned to `pair1` so later paths avoid its real terminals). Still never drops a color; healthy grids pay attempt 0 only. 4-path backtracker untouched (already pair-retries). Watch build times if quality is scarce at some size; next knob if simple shapes persist: MIN_BEND_FRACTION (2 bends passes a 9-cell path).
- Also: title brackets +0.025em down (`--bracketDrop` on .titleFrame, single knob); #solveTransition gained `max-width: min(90vw, 34rem)` (moves line is now its longest string — was unbounded).
- Front-end only; no back-end changes. NOT node-checked (no JS runtime; python brace/paren balance + progression sims only) and not browser-verified (standing rule). Test recipe: fresh profile → fast track L3 (first gate), L4/6/8 tier-ups, L9→10 hand-off (cue, instant 6×6 quad, HUD "Quad · 1 path"), PotD nudge at L5; menu Quad Marathon P1 = 6×6 + 1 gate; Zen solve popup moves line (+ Perfect on a min solve); batch of 3-path boards → no border-huggers.

## 2026-07-26 — Release v1.15 (added-path length floor)
- **Short "squiggle" secondary paths fixed at the ranking level** (user screenshot: ~6-cell yellow path on a multi-path board): added paths (colors 2-4) were scored on bends/interior/crossing but NEVER on length — the only gate was the hard `MIN_PATH_CELLS = 4`, so a tiny path with 2 bends + 2 interior cells scored a PERFECT 5/5 and won `tryAddPath`'s early-out. Path 1 was immune (its cap preference pressures length); the added colors had zero length pressure.
- New `addedPathMinCells()` = 60% of the path-1 floor (≈8 cells on 6×6, ≈12 on 9×9), scored at weight 2 (max 7, early-out at 7) in linear placement AND in the 4-path backtracker's budget gate (`BT_QUALITY_MIN` 3→5 of 7 = at least two of length/bends/interior). Still preference-with-fallback, never a hard reject — a short path beats dropping the color.
- Applies to all generation paths (marathon/practice, worker, PotD) — PotD boards already seeded keep their old paths, same rollout shape as the gate-count change.
- Same session, back-end (deploy pending): CORS regex for `*.game-files.crazygames.com` (CG re-uploads land on random-suffix subdomains; the static env allowlist silently broke PotD sync/tracking/leaderboards on CG — including Recent Visits showing nothing from CG previews).
- Version bumped 1.14 → 1.15.

## 2026-07-26 — Release v1.14 (circuit wording + lock-tip/tutorial race fix)
- **tutorial.intro wording**: "close the loop" → "complete the circuit" across all 15 languages, each phrased to match its `tooltip.firstPlay` sibling (de "Stromkreis schließen", ru "замкнуть цепь", etc.) — tooltip and tutorial now use identical vocabulary.
- **Lock-tip popped up right after finishing the How-to-Solve tutorial** (user hit it): the 30s timer fired under the tutorial overlay (marathon state stays PLAYING there; watched flag only sets at the FINAL step), and `isSeen` was only checked at queue time. Three-layer fix: (1) timer callback bails when `Tutorial.isOpen()`; (2) `Tooltip.processQueue` re-checks `isSeen` at display time and drops stale entries; (3) `Tutorial.close()` calls `Tooltip.dismissActive(false)` so nothing lingers under the overlay — un-seen, so unacknowledged non-tutorial tips still re-queue next puzzle.
- Test recipe: fresh flags (`_tooltipSeen_lockTile` + `_tutorialWatched`), open tutorial within 30s of a puzzle, dawdle past 30s, finish → no tip on close or later. Early-close variant: X out partway → no tip immediately, but it SHOULD return next puzzle.
- Version bumped 1.13 → 1.14.

## 2026-07-26 — Release v1.13 (tracking robustness, first-play tooltip, gate retunes, SW reload veto)
- **Tracking delivery robustness** (root cause of the admin's all-empty M/S/F iOS rows): funnel milestones now persist to a localStorage queue (`_pendingMilestones_v1`), cleared only on confirmed PATCH delivery; leftovers flush next load to the NEW `POST /api/visit/late-milestones` keyed by a client-minted per-load UUID (+ session_id fallback). `keepalive:true` on the visit POST + all PATCHes. **DEPLOY THE BACK-END FIRST** — until then flush POSTs 404 and their queue entries are lost (status-quo loss, but avoid).
- **firstPlay tooltip** ("Twist the tiles…", all 15 langs) on the auto-start run, with a How-to-Solve action button (reuses tutorial.menuButton); the in-HUD How-to-Solve button stays hidden until the tip is acknowledged (`Tooltip.showOnce` grew a 4th param `onAcknowledge` — fires on either button, NOT on dismiss-by-transition).
- **Gate retunes** (three progression iterations this session — user tuning by feel): final formula `1 + floor(solveCount/4)` (marathon/practice; was flat 4 + 1-per-3); PotD fixed 4 → 3 (new seeds only — shared boards already seeded keep 4); placement now skips ALL 8 vertices around a placed gate (Chebyshev 1 — orthogonal pairs touch tip-to-base, diagonal pairs form a near-touching L, screenshot-confirmed).
- **PotD start-token retries**: `postStart` was the last un-retried fetch and its loss = unrecoverable leaderboard day (confirmed in the wild: first-solver-of-the-day missing from an EMPTY s1 board — Render 502 during cold wake). Now 3 attempts [5s,12s,25s] + 750ms delays, retrying only 5xx/network/timeout; 404 still routes to re-seed recovery; failures log loudly. `postSubmit` still single-shot ON PURPOSE — needs server-side idempotency before retry is safe (double-insert risk).
- **Intro-music policy reversal**: admin edits to the intro list NO LONGER restart every player's curated sequence (this was why repeat visitors' replays sounded like new players — replays script the SUBJECT's recorded songs). `syncIntroState` now reconciles: old-fingerprint-minus-remaining = played, stays played; new songs queue at curated position. Universal CLAUDE.md 7b updated; TANTЯO port spawned as separate task.
- **SW auto-reload interaction veto**: ANY interaction (pointerdown/keydown/touchstart/gamepadconnected, once-capture) permanently vetoes the update auto-reload for that page load — the old "if user hasn't started anything yet" comment was never implemented, and a deploy reloaded the user's live PotD solve. PITFALL learned: `importScripts('/config.js')` means a config.js byte change triggers the SW update flow even with NO version bump (browsers byte-compare imported scripts too).
- Version bumped 1.12 → 1.13.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.12 (game-over PotD hook: save-first fix)
- **The v1.08 PotD hook was a silent score-discarder for ranking players** (user nearly lost a rank-2 run to it): for top-N players the card deliberately hides Back-to-menu so Save is the ONLY exit — the hook quietly added a second exit that navigated without submitting.
- Now SAVE-FIRST: when the name row is visible, hook click calls saveScore() (same semantics as Save — input name, empty → Anonymous, name persisted per rule 7a) then navigates to menu + PotD tab. Works because saveScore's synchronous head (read input, persist name, build payload) runs before goToMenu flips state, and its post-submit UI hops are already state-guarded — the POST proceeds in background. Non-ranking/zero-score players (row hidden) get plain navigation — deliberately NO saveScore call, since below-top-20 runs were never submitted and the hook shouldn't create submissions the Save-less card never made.
- Invariant to protect (noted in the handler comment): any future exit added to the game-over card while the name row is visible must save first.
- Test: rank on a Marathon board, type a name, click the hook instead of Save → entry appears on the board under that name.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.11 (replay exit silences SFX)

The replay-end continuation in `startReplayWithEvents` handled the MUSIC handoff only; now it also runs `Sfx.stopAllLoops()` + `Sfx.fadeOneShots()` — same cleanup quitToMenu does for live play.
- Single choke point covers all four exits (Stop mid-replay, Stop during hold, quit-to-menu, natural finish). Deliberately placed AFTER the finished-replay hold: a natural finish keeps its applause on the lingering solved frame (no loop survives a solve anyway — cleanup is a no-op there).

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.10 (tier retune: 5/3 → 4/1, same-size transitions)
- **TIER_LENGTH 5→4, TIER_DROP 3→1** (user insight: more paths on a SMALLER grid stacks two easing effects — fewer tiles AND higher path density → fewer filler decoys, more doubly-constrained tiles — so the old +1-path/−2-sizes transition was a difficulty DIP, not a step up). With DROP 1 the accumulator drop cancels the level's +1: each tier opens at the SAME size as the last puzzle solved — "same board, one more color". Size = monotonic difficulty axis; paths = variety axis.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.07 (quality floors extended into the 4-path backtracker)
- **v1.06's backtracker exemption didn't hold**: user deployed, played a 4-path quad board, and HINT-confirmed the intended solution still contained a simple (windy-but-border, crossing-free) path — the inward DFS bias alone shifts odds but the backtracker took the FIRST route that fit.
- **Budget-gated quality in place()**: while budget.steps > `BT_QUALITY_RESERVE` (80 of 200), a fitted route must score ≥ `BT_QUALITY_MIN` (3) on the shared floors (bends 2 + interior 2 + crossing 1) or it's skipped; once the budget dips into the reserve the gate opens and anything valid is accepted. Guarantees intact: four plain paths still beat three good ones, and worst-case build time is unchanged (same 200-step cap — the gate only changes which routes spend it).
- MIN=3 semantics: every path must at least (interior AND cross) or (windy AND cross) or (windy AND interior) while the search can afford it. Both user-reported simple shapes score 0-2.
- Knobs: BT_QUALITY_MIN (4 = windy+interior mandatory — likely too strict), BT_QUALITY_RESERVE (raise → less choosy → faster if 4-path builds ever feel slow).
- Supersedes v1.06's "backtracker exempt" note; maze.js comment rewritten with the history. Test recipe: fresh 4-path quad boards + HINT-check that intended paths wander interior and cross.
- Version bumped 1.06 → 1.07.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.06 (path quality: bend/interior/crossing floors + inward DFS bias)
- **Why (two user reports)**: (1) a near-straight border path (3 bends in ~20 cells) played trivially; (2) after the bend floor, two windy-but-still-simple paths — S-curving along the BORDER, crossing nothing — showed the other two easy shapes.
- **maze.js quality system** (single source — worker imports it, so Zen/Marathon pre-gen + urgent builds + PotD seeding all covered):
  - Bend floor: ≥25% of a path's cells must be elbows, min 2 (`MIN_BEND_FRACTION`/`MIN_PATH_BENDS`/`pathBendCountIn`).
  - Interior floor: ≥25% of cells at border-distance ≥2 (`INTERIOR_FRACTION`, `meetsInteriorFloor`); vacuous when the grid has <8 interior cells (`MIN_INTERIOR_AVAILABLE`) so tiny tier-1 boards are untouched.
  - Crossing preference (paths 2-3, linear mode): candidates sharing ≥1 cross-cell with an earlier path score higher (`pathCrossCountIn`).
  - Selection is SCORE-BASED, never rejecting: path 1 = bends(2)+interior(2), ties broken by the original length prefs (`betterPath1`); added paths = +crossing(1), early-out at 5. Worst case = best available candidate → build success rates unchanged.
  - **4-path backtracker exempt** from post-hoc floors (budget spent on fitting 4 paths; rejection risks dropped colors) — it gets quality via the new INWARD DFS BIAS in generateLanes: virgin exits that move deeper are usually tried first (60/40 coin-flip softening). Applies to every mode for free.
- Tuning knobs: INTERIOR_FRACTION, MIN_INTERIOR_AVAILABLE, MIN_BEND_FRACTION, the 0.6 bias probability (inline in dfs). Watch in testing: center-crowding, and 4-path quad build times staying prompt.
- NOT node-checked (no JS runtime) / not browser-verified (standing rule). One transient garbled comparison in betterPath1 was introduced and fixed within the session — worth an eyeball that path-1 sizes still prefer the cap correctly.
- Version bumped 1.05 → 1.06.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.04 (tracking opt-out hardening)
- **stickyOptOut bug fixed** (tracking.js): the v1.03 version wrapped everything in one try/catch — in contexts where localStorage THROWS (sandboxed/file:// pages, i.e. the editor preview pane), an explicit ?track=false load fell into the catch and tracked anyway. Now the query param decides the current load unconditionally; storage is persistence only.
- **Back-end belt-and-braces** (deploy circuitousness-api): `_is_localhost_origin` now also drops file:// visits — literal `Origin: null` header or file:-scheme origin/referrer. Real players always carry the site's https origin on these cross-host POSTs; ABSENT Origin is deliberately NOT dropped (curl tests survive). This guard works regardless of any browser's opt-out state — the durable fix for preview-pane pollution.
- Context: one more (direct) row slipped through on 7/23 — the pane's auto-reopen fired BEFORE the flagged navigate set its sticky flag. Wiped via /api/admin/wipe-session-visits. Preview pane's profile now carries the sticky flag.

## 2026-07-23 — Release v1.03 (phone leaderboard scrollbar fix + sticky ?track=false)
- **Portrait leaderboard h-scrollbar fixed**: the v1.00 widening set children to 94vw, but #leaderboard's 3vw-per-side padding makes its content box EXACTLY 94vw — zero slack, sub-pixel rounding spawned a page-wide scrollbar. Now `min(92vw, 32rem)`. User verified the fix on-device (file-only deploy + desktop-mode-toggle refresh trick) BEFORE this bump — this release is what actually delivers it to other players' PWA caches.
- **Lesson recorded**: file-only Netlify deploys are invisible to phone/PWA clients (service worker serves the APP_VERSION-keyed cache; desktop hard reload bypasses the SW, phones don't) — CSS/JS changes need the version bump to reach devices.
- **Sticky ?track=false opt-out** (tracking.js): the universal flag now works here AND persists per-browser via localStorage (`_trackOptOut_v1`; ?track=true clears). WHY: this project only honored per-load ?dev=true, and the editor's preview pane auto-reopens index.html WITHOUT params on every edit — a session of those loads posted real "(direct)" desktop/Windows rows on 7/23. Companion back-end endpoint `POST /api/admin/wipe-session-visits {sessionPrefix}` (8+ hex chars) added for surgical cleanup — user wiped the polluted session with it.

## 2026-07-22 — Release v0.98 (replay dead-air cap)
- **Replays cap inter-move dead air at 15s** (`REPLAY_MAX_GAP_MS`, config.js): new `compressReplayTimeline` in game.js runs before `playOneRecording`'s loop — pacing under the cap untouched, gaps beyond it play as exactly 15s. Quit/resume gaps were ALREADY edited out at resume time (`restoreRecording` rebases startTime — v0.93); this cap covers in-session idling, which PotD's uncapped clock makes unbounded.

Also `musicEvents` are remapped through the SAME piecewise map — otherwise song cues would fire after a compressed replay already ended (breaking v0.93's scripted playback on any long-think recording). Source recording never mutated (own-replay button plays the live object).

## 2026-07-22 — Release v0.96 (progressive Zen/Marathon revamp + PotD all-time boards)
- **⚠ DEPLOY ORDER: back-end FIRST.** app.py gained `/api/potd/alltime` (Streak/Career boards) AND `VALID_MARATHON_TYPES = {'s','q'} ∪ legacy 8` on the three marathon endpoints. A v0.96 front-end against the old back-end can't start runs, submit, or fetch the new boards. Old clients keep working (legacy union).
- **Zen/Marathon collapsed 8 types → 2 progressive types** ('s'/'q'): start 4×4 logical 1-path, +1 axis per solve, every TIER_LENGTH(5) puzzles path count steps up (→4) and growth drops back TIER_DROP(3) steps (retraces the same rolled axis entries — same dims again, deliberate). L16+ endless at 4 paths. Pure function of level (`pathCountForLevel`/`growthStepsForLevel` in marathon.js); tune via MARATHON.TIER_LENGTH/TIER_DROP/MAX_PATHS. Singular start 5×5→4×4 (PotD unaffected — own generateDims).
- **Pre-gen at tier boundaries** (the subtle part): upcomingDims entries now carry per-level pathCount/quadMode; fillPreGenQueue keys+builds with them; game.js startPuzzle callback's `invalidatePreGen` now fires ONLY on quad change (path changes are routine tier-ups — wiping would kill the just-built next-tier entries; cacheKey fully qualifies so stale entries can't be returned). Test: L5→L6 must show NO "Building puzzle…".
- **Legacy compat**: old saved runs ('s2' etc.) resume with fixed path count (decodeType legacy branch; save regex now `/^[sq][1-4]?$/`). Boundary resumes of legacy singular runs rebuild from the new 4×4 base (one-time, cosmetic). Old s1..q4 marathon boards UI-unreachable, server data intact.
- **Menu**: 2 progressive cards (#menuMarathonTypes, marathon.singular/quad keys now live) for Zen/Marathon; 8 slot cards (.menuPotdSlots) PotD-only via body.mode-potd CSS. Thumbs: 's'→1x1.png, 'q'→4x1.png. Solve popup gains #solveTierUp cue ("Paths increase to {n}!", new key ×15). HUD/continue labels reuse modeS1..modeQ4 keys with computed tier digit — zero new keys there.
- **Leaderboards**: marathon = 2 boards ('s'/'q', tile row only — path row is PotD-only now, CSS-hidden via lbModePotd absence). PotD gained Streak/Career chips IN the path row (types 'sstreak'/'qcareer' → /potd/alltime?set=&kind=; per-set, keyed by session_id, display name = latest non-Anonymous, retroactive from all history). Server dedups all-time rows by casefolded name (best entry per name — dev's fresh-cache testing filled 6/20 slots as "Digeratist"; stats stay per-session_id, NOT merged, to block name-impersonation).
- **Mode tabs reordered**: Zen · Marathon · PotD (user preference).
- Same-day follow-on to v0.95 (menu title/tabs spacer anchor — entry below); if 0.95 was never deployed, everything since 0.94 ships here.
- Tuning knobs flagged post-playtest: first singular Marathon puzzle grants exactly TIME_FLOOR 30s (4×4×1.875); per-tier START_TIME jumps inherited from per-type tuning.
- NOT node-checked (no JS runtime) and not browser-verified (standing rule — user tests). app.py py_compile OK. Key test recipe: L5→L6 tier-up (instant build, cue line, 6×6→5×5+2nd path), resume across boundary, legacy save resume, marathon board fetch ('s'), PotD Streak/Career boards, tab order.

## 2026-07-22 — Release v0.95 (menu title/tabs no longer jump on tab switch)
- **Menu vertical-centering rework** (user: selecting the PotD tab made the title + mode tabs jump up — the streak strip's extra height re-centered the whole stack): the `margin-top:auto` (titleFrame) / `margin-bottom:auto` (legal footer) spare-space-splitting pair is replaced by a `#menu::before` flex spacer with fixed basis `clamp(0rem, 7vh, 4.5rem)`. Title/tabs now sit at a constant top offset; tab switches only move content below; spare space collects at the bottom.

- Side effect to eyeball: on tall windows the menu sits a bit higher than the old dead-center. The spacer's `7vh`/`4.5rem` is the one knob if it needs lowering.
- Not browser-verified (standing rule — user tests).

## 2026-07-22 — Release v0.94 (supersedes unshipped v0.93 — full retention pass ships here)
- **v0.93 was never zipped/deployed** — this bump rolls it plus all same-day follow-ups into one release. The complete change list lives in the v0.93 entry below (streaks + calendar + countdown + saved-run resume + PotD resume + all fixes); everything below this line ships for the first time in 0.94.
- Post-0.93-entry additions covered by that entry's amendments: PotD quit=pause + ⏸ resume badge + wall-clock rule, streak-strip ✅ n/8 today-chip + calendar per-day counts, first-solve double-count fix (recount from slot markers + reconcileToday self-heal), chip explainer bubbles (hover on desktop replacing native titles / tap on touch), Pause/Quit labels in all three modes, ≤700px Quit→top-left, cursor + bubble-width polish.

## 2026-07-22 — Release v0.93 (retention pass: streaks, calendar, countdown, saved-run resume)
- **Context: CG Basic Launch ended below the bar** (desktop 8/15, mobile 6/15; D1 retention the weak metric — 1.65% desktop vs their 10–15% target). No official email yet; docs say verdict comes by email and resubmission after "meaningful improvements" is allowed. This release is the retention ammunition for the second-Basic-Launch request (see 07-20 entry's outreach plan).
- **PotD streaks** (new `potd-streaks.js`): 🔥 = consecutive UTC days with ≥1 slot solved (ANY solve counts, even ineligible retries — habit metric, not leaderboard); ⭐ = days with ≥1 hint-free solve. Record: `_potd_streak_v1` `{days:{date:{s,p}}, best, pBest, bf}`. One-time backfill (`bf` flag) from the legacy never-pruned `_potd_<date>_<slot>='solved'` keys — pre-feature players keep credit; backfilled days can't be ⭐. Streak alive through "yesterday" (Wordle convention). Menu strip (body.mode-potd-gated) + calendar 📅 + h:mm:ss countdown to UTC midnight, 1s tick.
- **Calendar** (new `potd-calendar.js`): month grid in the shared combo-modal shell; month/weekday names via Intl in the current i18n lang (only title/legend/button needed the 15-language treatment). Monday-first fixed. Forward nav capped at current month.
- **Saved-run resume** (marathon.js): one slot PER MODE (`_run_save_<mode>_v1`) so Marathon can't clobber a long Zen run. Checkpoints: onPuzzleReady, onSolve (boundary), Quit, pagehide/visibility-hidden. Mid-puzzle saves carry maze+gates+locked+clock+in-progress recording — exact restore, so quit/resume can't retry-scum. Mid-BUILD saves roll back level+fresh-time grant (startNextPuzzle already ++'d) and save as boundary. Cleared on gameOver + on fresh startGame of the same mode. Auto-start first-visit run never saves. Zen saves drop the completed-puzzle recordings array (never submitted; Zen runs are the long ones). New `Game.restoreRecording(rec)` in game.js rebases startTime to last move's t so the away-gap vanishes from replays; MUST be called after Maze.loadSnapshot (undo anchors on live board).
- **Resume eligibility**: stored sessionToken rides along — resumes within the server's 6h MAX_SESSION_MS submit normally; older ones fall to the existing local-board path. Fixed latent retry-queue bug alongside: submitToServer now attaches err.status, and both saveScore + flushPending DROP 4xx-rejected payloads instead of requeueing forever (any >6h run hit this pre-resume too).
- **Solve modal** gets 🔥 streak line + ⭐ earned/tease line (tease = near-miss hook when hints were used). i18n: 13 new keys × all 15 languages (register matched per locale).
- **CSS pitfall hit**: `.continueRunCard{display:flex}` silently defeats the `hidden` attribute (author rules beat the UA `[hidden]` rule regardless of specificity) — explicit `[hidden]{display:none}` override added. Remember for any future JS-toggled element that gets a display rule.
- **HUD quit label** is `marathon.pauseQuit` ("Pause/Quit", ×15 langs) in Marathon, Zen, AND PotD (potd.js startPuzzle/resumeAttempt set it; user caught PotD still saying QUIT after resume shipped) — quit checkpoints everywhere now. goToMenu still restores plain marathon.quit; every game entry re-asserts its own label. Auto-start run keeps "📅 Daily & More".
- **Chip explainer bubbles** (user: chips need tooltips; mobile has no hover so title attrs were invisible): 🔥/⭐/✅ chips are now `<button>`s sharing one popover under the strip (#potdStreakTip, created lazily in potd-streaks.js) with the same strings the title attrs used (no new keys). **Mode split at init via matchMedia(hover+fine)**: desktop = mouseenter/focus shows + mouseleave/blur hides (user follow-up: custom bubble REPLACES the native tooltip — title attributes removed from all four chips, aria-labels remain), no timeout; touch = tap toggles + tap-elsewhere + 6s timeout. The two wirings must never coexist (touch taps synthesize mouseenter → the click toggle would instantly hide). 📅 chip: hover-explainer only, excluded from tap mode (its tap opens the calendar — bubble would pop behind the overlay). Cursor: stat chips `default` (desktop click is a no-op — hover does the work), only 📅 keeps `pointer`. Tooltip.js deliberately NOT reused — once-per-key seen-flag shape.
- **Double-count bug fixed** (user: 3 solves showed ✅ 4/8): onSolve writes the slot's 'solved' marker BEFORE PotdStreaks.recordSolve, so the lazy backfill's first-ever run (triggered inside that first recordSolve) counted the just-written marker, then recordSolve incremented again — first solve = +2. Fix: `s` is now RECOUNTED from the per-slot markers (`countSolvedSlots`, ground truth, idempotent) instead of incremented, and `reconcileToday()` runs at init + day rollover — persists the backfill flag before any solve can race it AND self-heals existing drift (user's 4→3 on next load). Markers carry no hint data, so `p` keeps its flag semantics untouched. Re-seed marker wipes now also recount downward (day can drop out of the streak — correct, those solves were invalidated).
- **Streak-strip disambiguation** (user solved 3 slots, read "🔥 1 · ⭐ 1" as broken solve-counting — the chips are DAY-streaks working as intended, but a bare number invites exactly that misread): streak chips now carry a localized day-unit ("1d"/"1j"/"1日", new `potd.streak.chipDays` ×15); new ✅ n/8 today-chip (`potd.streak.todayTitle` ×15; total from Potd.SLOTS.length) shows the per-day solve count, which was always recorded (`days[date].s`) but never displayed; calendar solved-cells now show that count as a bottom-right mini badge (⭐ keeps top-right). Perfect (`p`) stays a per-day FLAG, not a count — deliberate, only the solved count was requested.
- **≤700px portrait: Quit moved to top-LEFT** (user phone screenshot: PAUSE/QUIT overlapped the centered timer at ~390px). CSS gate `#hudHowToBtn[hidden] ~ #hudQuitBtn` in the width-only max-width:700px block (same block as the Undo/Hint corner reshuffle — covers portrait phones plus any narrow window) — the one run where How-to owns top-left (first-visit auto-start) is Practice with a hidden timer, so its quit safely keeps the right-side flow slot. Left slot is free at this width (#hudLevel already lives below the strip).
- **PotD resume** (user follow-up, same release): quit/pagehide mid-solve checkpoints to `_potd_resume_<date>_<slot>` (the `resume_` infix keeps it clear of potd-streaks' backfill regex). Slot card shows a blue ⏸ badge (state 'started' + save present; refreshMenuIndicators keeps classes exclusive; `hasResume` is a parse-free existence probe — refresh runs often). Click resumes: board+recording+hintsUsed restored, ORIGINAL sessionToken reused (no /potd/start re-hit → eligibility survives). **WALL-CLOCK by user decision**: puzzleStartMs restores to the original epoch, away time counts — a paused clock would be a free think button on a time-ranked board (marathon's exact-clock restore differs on purpose: countdown = resource, not score). Saves >6h (server MAX_SESSION_MS) are discarded → normal retry flow. guardWatch now warns on watch when a resumable save exists (new `potd.watchWarn.bodyInProgress` ×15, body swapped via new #potdWatchWarnBody id) and discards the save on confirm; reconcileGeneratedAt re-seed wipes + day rollover prune the saves too.
- NOT node-checked (no JS runtime on PATH) and not browser-verified (standing rule — user tests). First-load console watch recommended. Test recipe: solve a PotD slot → streak line + strip + calendar; quit Zen mid-puzzle → Continue card restores exact board/clock; fresh run of same mode clears the card.
- Version bumped 0.92 → 0.93.

## 2026-07-21 — Release v0.92 (intro Full Screen checkbox deferred to AGREE)
- The intro's Full Screen checkbox no longer enters/exits fullscreen on click — it only records intent; `dismiss()` applies it (AGREE / Enter / Escape all count, and all are user gestures so Safari/iOS's synchronous-gesture requirement still holds).
- `dismiss()` only acts when checkbox state differs from the document's actual fullscreen state, so an untouched box is a no-op; the F11-sync listener on the checkbox stays.
- Version bumped 0.91 → 0.92.

## 2026-07-19 — Release v0.90 (new icon set + debug-mode keyboard tools)
- **All 11 `/icons` PNGs + `favicon.svg` replaced with the new title-O icon** (red ring on 2×2 tiles, white viewfinder brackets, transparent bg). Source: `icon-o-tuner.html` → user-tuned export at `Downloads\icon-o-512.png`. Maskable pair flattened onto opaque `#0a0a2e` (Android fills maskable transparency with white); regular icons keep transparency (iOS apple-touch-icon black-fills — accepted). favicon.svg = SVG-wrapped base64 96px PNG, same filename so index.html/sw.js untouched.
- **`icon-o-tuner.html` created** (temp dev tool, zip-EXCLUDED — added to CLAUDE.md's exclude list): renders the title-O through the real pipeline (title-renderer.js's snapshot → renderSnippet → paintSnippetRingMask) with `litPulse:false` = exactly halfway flash brightness (pulse is 1 ± 0.45·sin). Sliders + PNG export. The older `title-o-tuner.html` it mimics no longer exists on disk.
- **Debug-mode keyboard extras** (index.html debug-sliders wiring, all gated per-keypress on `mode-debug`, zero game-mode effect): ESC toggles `.dbgHidden` on `<html>` (hides #debugSliders + #hintBtn + #undoBtn via one CSS rule); ArrowRight/ArrowLeft step the background pool SEQUENTIALLY ±1 with wrap via new `Render.debugCycleBackground(dir)` (logs `[dev] background → levelN.jpg`); plain F5 = rebuildGrid() with current slider params (Ctrl/Shift+F5 fall through for real reloads). Arrows/F5 skip focused inputs per-key (arrows must nudge sliders; F5 doesn't care).
- **PITFALL hit: `const Render` is NOT `window.Render`** — top-level const never attaches to window, so a `window.Render &&` guard silently no-ops forever. game.js works because it explicitly assigns `window.Game`. Comment now marks the spot in the wiring script.
- Deploy facts confirmed this session: git deploy serves `/icons/*` fine (bare `/icons` 404 is just no-directory-listing); itch's iframe can never PWA-install (install criteria evaluate the top-level itch.io page), so installed users exist only on the real domain; Android WebAPK re-checks manifest+icon bytes ~daily on launch, iOS home-screen icons are frozen until delete-and-re-add.
- Version bumped 0.89 → 0.90.

## 2026-07-17 — Release v0.89 (audio moved to the shared music host)
- **All audio now loads from `https://music.official-intelligence.art`** (the new `oi-music/` Netlify project at the umbrella root — see its CLAUDE.md/NOTES.md). WHY: Render workspace bandwidth hit 23.99/25 GB with ~10 days left in the month; the culprit was iOS music + all-platform SFX streaming through the back-end `/api/music/` proxy. Netlify Pro's credit pool absorbs the load ~5×.
- config.js: new `MUSIC_HOST` const; `MUSIC_BASE_URL` is unconditional now (the `IS_IOS_AUDIO` URL split is gone — the host serves direct 200s + CORS on every platform). `IS_IOS_AUDIO` itself stays: music.js still needs it for the gesture-blessing workaround. audio.js SFX base → `MUSIC_HOST + '/SFX/' + PROJECT_SLUG + '/'`.
- **Back-end `/api/music/` proxy left alive but idle on purpose** — old cached PWA clients keep calling it until they self-update. Retire it in a later session once Render's bandwidth graph shows that traffic dead.
- `You Heard About Pluto_.mp3` was broken in the songs DB (space-name, no matching release asset — never played in-game); user fixed the filename in the admin songs tool during the migration.
- Same-day follow-up to v0.87 (below), driven by the user's phone-screenshot testing. All v0.87 deploy TODOs still stand (CG portal portrait flag; PotD sizes at next seed).
- **iOS bless bug — REAL-DEVICE BUG, not just emulation** (music.js `blessOnGesture`): gesture #1's bless ran before any src existed (play() rejected → `blessed` reset for retry) while the same gesture's bubble-phase kick started a real song; gesture #2's retry then ran the silent play()/pause() dance on the PLAYING element and its `.then()` pause KILLED the music. Fix: `!a.paused` early-out (already playing ⇒ already blessed). Symptom was "music starts on first tap, dies on the next"; may have depressed iOS solve-audio stats.
- **⏮ resurfaced the menu-only track in-game** (music.js): prev-history was global across phases. `setMenuPhase` now clears `history` on actual phase FLIPS only (guarded — repeated same-phase goToMenu calls must not wipe within-phase history), mirroring startCreditsSequence's precedent. Carried-over song still plays through; ⏮ on it restarts it. stop()'s "history preserved" comment cross-references this so they don't read as contradictory.
- **Now Playing chip: bottom-CENTER on ALL devices/orientations** (base `#nowPlaying`; was bottom-right), and **title-sized everywhere** — `max-width: calc(100vw - 1.5rem)` replaced the 26vmin clamp (ellipsized long titles on desktop too). Box stays shrink-to-fit; corner controls (z-25) always render above it (chip z-24, pointer-transparent except transport).
- **Canvas reserves the chip's band** (render.js resize): half of (chip height + ~0.8rem offset), max()'d with the ≤700px corner-button reserve (shared band), margin-top = DIFFERENCE of top/bottom reserves. `resize` is now EXPORTED; music.js's song-change handler calls it on visibility FLIPS (mid-game mute/unmute re-centers live). **Predictive reserve**: chip hidden but `!Music.isMuted()` → chip is coming after first gesture; measured via no-paint trick (inline visibility:hidden + .visible toggled inside one synchronous layout pass; empty title padded with a literal NBSP — plain space collapses to no line box). Kills the first-click jump. Accepted: music-on + dead song API = ~35px reserved for a chip that never comes.
- **≤700px HUD reshuffle**: How-to → TOP-LEFT (the level readout's old slot; bottom-center now belongs to the chip), `#hudLevel` → dropped below the strip, centered (`top: calc(100% + 0.4rem)`) — hangs into the canvas top pad, accepted. Top-strip overlap fix (portrait block): How-to/Quit padding 0.9→0.5rem, gear 0.45rem, #hud gap 0.5rem, How-to letter-spacing 0.12→0.06em (0.12em padded the ❓-to-text space wider than Quit's 📅).
- **Tutorial bullets are display:none until revealed** (was opacity-0 with height reserved → scrollbar visible before any step on portrait). Two-stage reveal in revealBullet (inline display first, `--in` next frame — same-frame flip skips the fade) + scrollIntoView keeps the newest bullet visible. TRADE-OFF FLIPPED knowingly: modal now grows per step (the old fixed-height design existed to prevent exactly that).
- **Leaderboard rows fit portrait** (end-of-file portrait block, banner now "MENU + LEADERBOARD"): rank col 2→1.6rem, gaps 0.8→0.4rem, hints nowrap, lbName min-width:0 + ellipsis (required for its 1fr track to shrink at all), 0.85rem font. Was: "2 hints" wrapped at the list's 20rem floor.
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user phone-tested each step via screenshots; the music fixes verified by user in iPhone emulation.
- Version bumped 0.87 → 0.88.

## 2026-07-17 — Release v0.87 (PORTRAIT SUPPORT + PotD ×0.75)
- **Why: CrazyGames mobile funnel** (conversion 44% / playtime 3m33s / D1 0% vs healthy desktop). Root cause: the rotate-device overlay walled off every portrait phone. Overlay RETIRED — its `@media` display-flip removed from styles.css; markup + `rotate.*` i18n keys + base CSS kept for one-query re-enable. `manifest.json` orientation → `any`. **User TODO on deploy: allow portrait in the CG portal's orientation setting.**
- **HUD ≤700px**: Undo → fixed bottom-LEFT corner, Hint → bottom-RIGHT (thumb-reach; the ±10rem center cluster hung off both edges below ~700px), first-run How-to → bottom-center (its center-top slot collides with Quit/⚙ at phone widths). `position: fixed` is viewport-anchored (no transformed ancestors) and render.js's hudReserve is unaffected (buttons were already out of flow). Accepted: rare visual overlap with bottom-edge terminal pads on tall late-run grids.
- **Marathon/Zen growth is viewport-keyed** (marathon.js ensureGrowthSequence): LONG-AXIS-first invariant — wider-first on landscape (unchanged), taller-first on portrait. Sampled per roll; already-rolled entries stay pinned (pre-gen lookahead). Replays unaffected (dims stored per puzzle).
- **Portrait-phone menu block at END of styles.css** (`max-width:600px and portrait` — orientation-gated so the max-height:500 landscape squeeze can't double-match; MUST stay last, wins by source order): title re-bound to vw (the tablet-class ~line-1266 block also fires on portrait phones and its 6.5vh title blew past 390px width), subtitle wraps (base is nowrap), card rows forced 4-across with vw thumbs (min-width 6.5rem/6rem had wrapped them 2×2 = menu twice as tall), bottom buttons compact (wrap = fallback for wordy locales), gear tucked. Mode tabs get content-width sizing in their own ≤600px block (equal flex:1 + nowrap clipped "📅 Puzzle of the Day").
- **Settings h-scrollbar fix (base rules, not media)**: `.settingsSelect min-width: 0` + `.settingsSlider min-width: 2rem`. PITFALL now documented in both comments: flex `min-width:auto` floors items at intrinsic width — a select's is its WIDEST OPTION (~280px), a range input's ~8rem; `flex: 1` alone does NOT let them shrink (the old comment claimed it did).
- **Credits portrait fixes**: `.credits-title font-size: min(36px, 7.5vw)` + tracking `min(0.3vh, 0.6vw)` (36px Orbitron ≈ 420px — clipped at 393px; ≥480px viewports render identical). Role/name columns 35vw → 44vw in the portrait block (""Slop, With Love" Lyrics" wrapped; symmetric widths keep the seam centered). Soundtrack pills compacted + row flex-wrap + nowrap labels; heading pinned nowrap at 13px / `min(2px, 0.5vw)` tracking ("SOUNDTRACK AVAILABLE / ON" must never break).
- **Tooltip narrow strip bottom 1rem → 4.75rem** (clears the new corner buttons + the bottom-right Now Playing chip — collision found in the popup audit).
- **PotD sizes ×0.75, ALL tiers** (potd.js): full tier landed exactly on the old LOW numbers → singular [6,9] avg [7,9], quad even [10,14] avg [11,14]; new LOW (1-2 paths) singular [5,7] avg [6,7], quad even [8,10] avg [9,10]. Floors still reject only min×min-ish rolls; max×max legal. Global (shared daily puzzle — can't be per-device). **Applies at the NEXT daily seed** (or dev PotD reset today).
- **iOS audio proxy: NO WORK NEEDED — already live.** `/api/music/<tag>/<filename>` on circuitousness-api + full front-end wiring (config.js IS_IOS_AUDIO, audio.js SFX-always) predate this session; verified in production with Range probes (206 + Content-Range + CORS on Music/Frenemy.mp3 and SFX/cinematic_bass_1.mp3). The v0.55-era "iOS WILL FAIL" NOTES entry far below is STALE — don't re-diagnose from it.
- Unchanged for portrait, deliberately: leaderboard (20rem floor fits ~352px+), tutorial (had its own ≤640px stack), settings vertical layout, PotD server-seeded grids (render smaller in portrait rather than transposing — replays/shared-daily forbid per-device dims).
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user drove testing via phone screenshots all session (menu, settings, credits verified iteratively; HUD corners + growth direction still to eyeball).
- Version bumped 0.86 → 0.87.

## 2026-07-16 — Release v0.86 (q4 starts 4×4 + ships the HUD-clearance layout)
- **Quad 4-path starts at 4×4 logical like every other quad count** (`MIN_DIM_QUAD_4PATH` 6 → 4 in config.js — third value for this knob: 8 → 6 → 4). User's rationale: 4×4 logical = 8×8 PHYSICAL maze, more room than the 5×5 physical grid the singular 4-path generator already handles reliably (the v0.83 Debug testing). STARTER_PLAN pre-gen + dimsForLevel follow automatically via startDimsFor/minDimFor; stale "q4 starts at 6" comments fixed in config.js / marathon.js / maze.js. Both 4PATH min-dim constants are now dead fallbacks (documented as such in config.js).
- **Also ships everything in the post-v0.85 entry below**: the half-HUD canvas clearance (terminal pads no longer under UNDO/HINT) and the heart-art-bg.jpg relocation.
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user tests. Test recipe: start q4, expect "Puzzle 1 · 4×4" with a prompt build; also re-check a 5×5 singular with a top-edge terminal for the HUD clearance.

## 2026-07-16 — Post-v0.85: HUD-clearance layout, heart-art bg restored (shipped in v0.86)
- **Canvas now clears the HUD buttons** (user screenshot: top terminal pad under UNDO): `resize()` measures `#hud`'s live height, subtracts HALF of it from availH and sets a matching canvas margin-top. Half, not full — full-height reserve was tried first and the user judged it moved the grid down too much; the canvas's own pad region covers the rest. `#hud` is measurable at resize time because marathon.js `showOnly(hudEl)` runs before the startPuzzle callback; hidden HUD (menu/debug) measures 0.
- **heart-art-bg.jpg was stranded in the project PARENT folder** (`circuitousness/`) — heart-art.html loads it same-directory and silently skips the backdrop when missing. Moved back next to heart-art.html. PITFALL: opening heart-art.html via `file://` taints the canvas once the bg draws (Chrome treats file-loaded images as cross-origin) → Download PNG silently dies in `toBlob`. Must serve via the dev server; the "same-origin → not tainted" comment in the file is only true over http.
- **Tracking semantics confirmed for the admin "Puzzles per Session" chart**: `Tracking.recordStart` fires once per GAME/run (startGame only — startNextPuzzle records nothing), so a multi-puzzle Zen run = one game_type entry. The chart was mislabeled; fixed on the back-end + OI-web admin (see circuitousness-back-end NOTES same date). No front-end change made — per-run start semantics kept deliberately (the funnel's "started" means "chose to play").
- Also this session, non-front-end: Path Four lyrics updated for strict-mode/terminal-pad/palette changes (Music/Lyrics/Path Four.txt); CrazyGames exclusivity checked (none for app stores — Google Play paid listing is fine, but free→paid is one-way on Play).

## 2026-07-16 — Release v0.85 (pre-won path guard + joined-flash matches title-O + 45° gate tips)
- **Puzzles can no longer START with a path already completed** (user screenshot: q4 start with a path pre-gold). Root cause: nothing re-checked `pathsWon` after scrambling — `scrambleQuads` gives each quad a 1-in-4 chance of 0 turns (symmetric quad contents survive non-zero turns too), and non-quad rerolls randomize tiles independently. New `breakPreWonPaths()` in maze.js, called at the END of all four newPuzzle branch tails after `updateHighlighted()` (quad / 2-path / 3+ / 1-path — the tails have DIFFERENT indentation, a replace-all initially missed two): while any real path is won (filter through entry2/3/4 existence — `pathsWon` is true for missing paths), nudge a random lit cell of that path and re-check (cap 40). Quad: +1..3 turns via rotateQuad + quadScramble bookkeeping + twin-quad lockstep (mirrors scrambleQuads). Non-quad: rotation bump (straight ±1 only — +2 is a symmetry no-op; crosses and entry/exit cells excluded), twin partner bumped in lockstep. Known accepted trade-off: non-quad nudge lands AFTER the canonical checks, so a rescued puzzle can be marginally off-canonical.
- **Joined-red flash now looks identical to the flashing title-O** (user noticed divergence — same litStripes/colors/pulse, but v0.84's O retune gave only the O the new ramp shape). litStripes ramp overrides are now THREE-level: palette-carried (new, wins) → snippet opts → LIT_* constants. `JOINED_RAMP` const in render.js (0.00/0.36/0.60/0.43) rides on litPalette's JOINED branch; MUST stay in sync with title-renderer.js litOpts (cross-referencing comments both sides).
- **Gate arrow tips are now exactly 45°** to match the tile-bevel miter: gates.js `TIP_LENGTH_FRAC` 0.135→0.06 (= SHAFT_WIDTH_FRAC/2 — relationship pinned in a comment), freed length into `SHAFT_LENGTH_FRAC` 0.755→0.83 so total reach stays 1.0 (tip still lands on the adjacent vertex; hitTest derives from the same constants). If the tip reads too squat, the retune lever is widening SHAFT_WIDTH_FRAC with TIP kept at half of it.
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user tests. Pre-won guard test recipe: Debug, quad+4 paths, small grid, restart repeatedly — no path should ever start gold.
- Version bumped 0.84 → 0.85.

## 2026-07-16 — Release v0.84 (promo-look retune: brushed metal, new palette defaults, title-O tuner)
- **Brushed-metal tile-face texture**: procedural seamless 256px pattern (two-scale horizontal box-blurred noise, `TEX_LAYERS` in render.js controls streak length/strength), overlaid per face polygon with `'overlay'` blending so it works on every face color; strength scales with face alpha. `Render.setFaceTexture(0..1)` + debug slider, default 0.66. Grain is screen-space (flows across quad-merged faces, spins with rotation anims).
- **Lit-gradient Mach-band fix**: litStripes' three linear ramp segments read as distinct bands (slope breaks at CORE_START/CORE_FULL); each segment's local t now runs through smoothstep (continuous derivative end-to-end) and `LIT_GRADIENT_STEPS` 14→20. User-visible smoothness was the goal — don't "simplify" back to linear.
- **All visual defaults re-tuned against the CG promo art** (user dialed in via debug panel): tile `#204169`, path `#ff0000`, width 0.17, face opacity 0.74; all five lit palettes replaced (green/blue/pink/orange/gold base+lo+hi — values in render.js). Key insight recorded: `base` (mid stop) dominates the tube AND feeds the glow pass; promo-matching required exposing it (new setters for all palettes' three stops; gold got a base setter too).
- **Existing players forced onto the new look**: the six visual Settings keys versioned to `_setting_*_v2` (old persisted values were tuned against the old palette); un-versioned keys purged at boot. Audio/name settings untouched.
- **Debug panel compacted**: tile|path pickers share a row, lit palettes are a 5-row × base/outer/inner grid with hex readouts, lock-face picker removed (API `Render.setLockFaceColor` kept, console-only).
- **Title-O**: new one-off `title-o-tuner.html` (heart-art-style page, live litOpts + copy block; zip-excluded in CLAUDE.md). render.js snippet mode gained ramp-SHAPE overrides (`edgeLift/coreStart/coreFull/coreHot` opts) because the fixed constants assume near-white `hi` stops — with the O's dark red palette they forced a stark thin white filament no color could soften. Shipped title-O ramp: 0.00/0.36/0.60/0.43 (in title-renderer.js litOpts; tuner Reset mirrors it).
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user live-tuned everything on the dev server and drove all values.
- Version bumped 0.83 → 0.84.

## 2026-07-15 — Release v0.83 (ALL singular starts unified at 5×5 + size-capped Marathon time)
- **All singular starts are now 5×5 for EVERY path count (1-4), both Zen and Marathon** (`startDimsFor` returns 5×5 for any `!quadMode`). Fourth retune of this knob. Trigger: user Debug-tested 4-path on 4×4 repeatedly — worked every time (few seconds of lag at worst), disproving config.js's "generator fails at 8×8" comment, which described the PRE-backtracking linear placement (see 2026-05-01 entry). `MIN_DIM_SINGULAR_4PATH=10` kept as a documented dead fallback; `MIN_DIM_PRACTICE_SINGULAR` table + the `practice` arg on `minDimFor`/`startDimsFor` REMOVED (Zen ≡ Marathon now — `dimsForLevel` no longer passes `isPractice`). Quad untouched (4 logical, 6 for q4): logical dims are 2× physical, so quad 4×4 is already an 8×8 maze and "5×5" would make quad starts BIGGER. Stale comments rewritten in config.js / marathon.js / game.js / maze.js.
- **Side effect worth knowing**: STARTER_PLAN's 8 pre-genned starters now serve Zen too (identical dims) — pre-unification, Zen 2/3-path always missed the starter cache.
- **Marathon fresh time now size-capped for singular** (user: "20 minutes banked within a few puzzles" after the small starts): new `TIME_PER_TILE_SINGULAR [1.875, 2.5, 3.125, 3.75]` s/tile in config.js; `timeForPuzzle(quad, paths, solved, logicalDims)` grants `max(TIME_FLOOR, min(schedule, perTile×tiles))`. Rates = old `START_TIME_SINGULAR/64` (the old 8×8 baseline; 4-path continues +0.625), so a 5×5 grants 47/63/78/94s and the cap stops binding ~level 5-6 — mid/late game identical to the old model, runs still guaranteed to end. Quad skips the cap (its starts never shrank). Solve-popup projection passes `dimsForLevel(level+1, …)` so the "carrying X" message matches what's actually granted.
- **Next knob if banking still balloons**: lower the `TIME_PER_TILE_SINGULAR` rates (stretches how long the cap binds); deliberately NOT capping banked total — bank-early-spend-late is the design.
- Not browser-verified (standing rule) — user tests.
- Version bumped 0.82 → 0.83.

## 2026-07-15 — Release v0.82 (gold paths pulse their glow)
- **Won (gold) paths now breathe their glow** (user request, mirroring the joined-red pulse): `strokeGlowLayer` modulates the GLOW_LAYERS alphas by a sine when `Maze.pathsWon[pathIdx]` — per-path, so in multi-path puzzles each path starts pulsing the moment IT completes. The crisp gold band stays steady (only the glow pulses); the joined-red flash still flickers band+glow via litPalette's color scaling — deliberate contrast: celebration vs alarm. Knobs: `GOLD_GLOW_PULSE_PERIOD_MS 1500` (matches hint-tile breathing, calmer than red's 900) / `GOLD_GLOW_PULSE_AMP 0.45`, above strokeGlowLayer.
- **Pulse driving**: `shouldKeepAnimating()` gained `anyPathWon()`, and `draw()` forces full redraws while gold is active (partial redraws would leave stale-brightness glow outside the dirty clip) — same pattern/cost class as the hint-tile pulse, which already runs rAF for a whole puzzle.
- **Pitfall encoded in `anyPathWon()`**: `Maze.pathsWon` reports `true` for NON-existent paths (so maze.js's overall-won AND works) — the helper filters through `collectPairs()`; a naive `.some(Boolean)` would have kept the rAF loop running forever in every mode.
- Same-day follow-up to v0.81's glow system; glow-tuning history and the erase pitfalls live in that entry below.
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user tests.
- Version bumped 0.81 → 0.82.

## 2026-07-15 — Release v0.81 (promo-art look: terminal endpoints + neon gradient + glow)
- **Square terminal endpoints are now the DEFAULT** (render.js `COMPLETE_CIRCUITS = false`): entry/exit stubs end in glowing square ring pads like the CrazyGames promo art, instead of wrapping the perimeter. The old wrap loop is intact behind `Render.setCompleteCircuits(true)` (console only — no settings UI). Terminal mode uses a fixed `TERMINAL_PAD_FRAC = 0.75` pad at ANY path count, so multi-path grids render larger than wrap mode did (no fan-out rings to reserve room for). Canvas no longer viewport-fills in non-wrap mode. Ring is stroked thinner than the path (`TERM_RING_STROKE_SCALE 0.72`) so the pad stays small without closing its dark hole; stub+ring stripes INTERLEAVE dark→bright so their hot cores connect at the T-junction (shared geometry in `terminalGeom`).
- **Lit-path gradient reshaped to a neon tube** (litStripes): non-linear ramp — lifted edges (`LIT_EDGE_LIFT 0.78`), base→hi ramp from `LIT_CORE_START 0.48`, thin white-hot filament past `LIT_CORE_FULL 0.84` pushing `LIT_CORE_HOT 0.80` toward white. These four constants + `GLOW_LAYERS` are THE tuning knobs; user iterated ~6 rounds against promo screenshots to land here.
- **New Pass E glow**: lit lanes + terminals cast color onto tile bevels. Layered wide additive strokes ('lighter'), NOT shadowBlur (too slow for rotation-anim partial redraws). Per path color, lanes+stub+ring trace as ONE union path on the offscreen (single stroke per layer = no additive stacking — separate strokes made terminals glow 2-3× and jump at the grid edge), then the crisp bands are erased (destination-out) at EXACT band width before compositing. Pitfalls fixed along the way, don't regress: erase caps must be BUTT (round caps gouged a void at dead-end lane ends); ring erase must match the band's ROUNDED silhouette via fill+round-join-stroke (sharp fillRect corners notched the glow); erase inset must be 0 (a 2px "tuck" showed as a 1px bright rim once glow alphas rose).
- `pathChannel` split into `laneGeom` (no beginPath) + wrapper so the glow pass can accumulate multi-tile union paths; `collectPairs()` factored out; tutorial default pad follows the mode (terminals fit the small canvas via terminalGeom's avail-pad clamping).
- Not browser-verified by Claude (standing rule) — user live-tested every round on the dev server and drove the tuning by screenshot comparison against the promo art.
- Version bumped 0.80 → 0.81.

## 2026-07-14 — Release v0.80 (auto-start no longer counts as a "start" until the player touches the puzzle)
- **First-visit auto-start now defers the `Tracking.recordStart` funnel milestone until the player's first committed puzzle action** (user request: a dumped-in player who never taps anything is not a "start"). `marathon.js` `startGame` parks `{mode, gameType}` in a new module-level `deferredStartTracking` slot when `isFirstRunAutoStart` is set (instead of PATCHing immediately); new public `Marathon.notifyPuzzleInteraction()` fires it one-shot; `goToMenu` nulls the slot so a no-touch bail (Quit / "📅 Daily & More") is never counted — the visit stays a reached-menu-only funnel row.
- **Hook point**: `game.js` `recordMove` (right after the `appendMove` guard) — the single funnel for every committed action (rotate / gate / lock / hint), unreachable by replays. Opening Settings or the How-to tutorial deliberately does NOT count. Normal starts (menu cards, PotD) record immediately as before.
- Funnel stays monotonic with no back-end change: auto-start players must interact before they can solve, so "started" still always precedes "finished".
- **Deliberately left alone**: CG SDK `gameplayStart` still fires in `onPuzzleReady` during the auto-start run — it's CG's loading/gameplay bracket, not our stats funnel.
- Not browser-verified (standing rule — user tests). Test recipe: fresh profile (or clear `circuitousness_firstVisitAutoStarted_v1` + `_mode` + `_lastPlayerName`), auto-start, quit untouched → admin shows reached-menu with no start; repeat with one tile rotation → start appears.
- Version bumped 0.79 → 0.80.
