## 2026-07-13 — Release v0.77 (tracking race fix + Instrumental Only + 6×6 starts)
- **Fixed the "more puzzles completed than started" digest anomaly** (7/12 email: 21 started / 30 completed). Root cause in `tracking.js`: `withVisit` DROPPED events fired while the initial `POST /visit` was still in flight (`recordVisit`'s in-flight guard returned visitId=0 immediately). On a cold Render dyno (free tier, 30-60s first response) every `/agreed`+`/started` in the first minute vanished; `/finished` minutes later landed. The first-visit AUTO-START made it worse — it moved `recordStart` from "human clicks a card" to milliseconds after intro-clear. Fix: `visitPromise` is stored and awaited, events queue until the visit row exists. Verified with a mocked 300ms-slow visit POST: start/finish fired mid-flight now land with the real id.
- The server-side backfill (finish → started_game boolean) had already kept the FUNNEL monotonic; the digest counts the `game_type` CSV vs `finished_count`, which the backfill can't patch — hence only the email showed the inversion. No back-end change needed.
- Ruled out for gameplay: dropping the paid Render tier can't glitch play — marathon/practice are fully client-side, PotD fetch has timeouts+retries+cache, tracking/leaderboards are fire-and-forget. CG's 13.33% desktop "crash rate" (+100% vs a 0 day) looks like a 2-in-~15-loads artifact; unrelated to cold starts (nothing blocks on the API). Watch it; chase only if the CG portal shows actual error details.
- **"Instrumental Only" music setting** (Settings toggle, under the music volume row; key `circuitousness_setting_musicInstrumentalOnly`). music.js filters every pool through `eligible()` — pool-accessor level, so bag fingerprints self-invalidate on toggle. Decisions: a list with NO instrumental tracks falls back to the full pool (setting can never silence the game); the curated intro is BYPASSED, not consumed, while on (resumes intact if toggled off); toggling on mid-lyrics-song skips immediately (replays exempt). Song cache bumped `_songLibrary_v6` (entries now carry `instrumental`). i18n `settings.instrumentalOnly` in all 15 langs. Server side: `Song.instrumental` flag + admin checkbox shipped separately (umbrella API + admin page, deployed 7/13); admin must flag songs before the toggle has anything to filter — until then the fallback plays everything.
- **Instrumental Only is grayed out + ignored in credits-including music modes** ('credits' / 'game_plus_credits' — the End Credits pool is all-lyrics, so honoring the toggle would contradict the mode choice). `instrumentalActive()` in music.js gates everything (eligible pools, intro bypass, toggle-on skip); Settings toggles `.disabled` on the row like the audience-reactions pattern; the stored preference survives and re-applies on switching back to Game Playlist. `setMode` now clears the menu/credits bags too when the flag is on (mode change flips pool shapes). Same feature mirrored in TANTЯO (`instrumentalIgnoredByMode()` off `currentMusicSelection`; checkbox.disabled + new `.settings-option.disabled` CSS). Both verified live incl. the game_plus_credits case: intro/album plays normally and lyrics songs stay reachable.
- **1-path starts are now 6×6 in BOTH Zen and Marathon** (`startDimsFor` collapsed to one return; was Zen 4×5 / Marathon 5×5 from the 7/12 retune). s1 pre-gen follows automatically (STARTER_PLAN reads the same function). Verified live: fresh-profile auto-start and a menu-started Marathon both show "Puzzle 1 · 6×6" with a 6×6 grid; quad Zen 4×4 / Zen 2-path 6×6 unchanged.
- Admin recent-visits "Src" column mislabeled ALL embeds as itch (payload only has an `embedded` boolean; label was hardcoded). Now inferred from referrer: cg/itch/embed. Umbrella-site fix (admin page), deployed 7/13 — noted here because the M/S/F + Src columns are how this project's funnel gets audited. `IS_CRAZYGAMES` (hostname-based) was never affected.
- Version bumped 0.76 → 0.77.
- **Every external anchor now `target="_blank" rel="noopener"`** (16 in index.html). The big catch: the Alan Deardorff Wikipedia credit (and the OI/TANTЯO credits links) had NO target — on CG a click would have navigated the game IFRAME itself to Wikipedia inside crazygames.com; on our own site it killed the game mid-credits. The `data-credit-link` tracking handler only records, never intercepts, so navigation always proceeded.
- **Rule assessment recorded** (user asked): Wikipedia bio / Suno attribution / Spotify+Apple soundtrack pages / ASPCA charity link are all judged low-risk on CG — the prohibition targets cross-promotion of games/platforms; none of these qualify, though none are in CG's explicit allow-list either (reviewer-discretion gray zone). CG's "App Store links never allowed" means app pages, not music catalog pages. TANTЯO link stays cg-hidden (real cross-promo). Worst case per CG's iterative QA process: a change request in the dev portal, not a ban (bans are for stolen builds/fraud).
- Also fixed the `</a -->` artifact left from v0.68's un-commenting of the Apple Music link (parsed harmlessly as a bogus end tag, but sloppy).
- **PotD nudge delayed to puzzle 3** (after the SECOND solve, was after the first): user judged it too aggressive so soon, given the "📅 Daily & More" button already advertises the daily on-screen for the whole run.
- Version bumped 0.75 → 0.76. Only `index.html` + `marathon.js` changed (+ version pair).
- **Perimeter ring pulled in**: render.js `notchLength()` 0.4 → 0.3 of cellSize — the single-path loop's top run grazed the HUD buttons on short-wide grids (observed on CG, 6×4 Zen puzzle). Notch grooves + route corners shrink in step (all keyed off the one constant). Arithmetic-verified (~12px clearance at the observed geometry); NOT injectable-testable (closure-private canvas logic) — eyeball on CG. If a graze ever recurs on tall marathon grids, the proper next step is reserving the HUD strip's height in Render.resize().
- **HUD button swap**: Quit/"📅 Daily & More" now left of the ⚙ Settings gear (markup order only — they're the two in-flow flex-end children).
- **CG container mute now PAUSES music and resumes where it left off** (was volume-0 while playback kept advancing — muted players silently burned through intro-sequence songs). `externalPaused` distinguishes our pause from the player's ⏯; auto-resume only if the player's own state still wants playback. The effectiveVolume() floor stays as belt-and-braces (anything starting playback WHILE muted plays silently — CG's mute-wins rule holds on every path). SFX unchanged (momentary one-shots; nothing to resume).
- **Solve-audio stat now counts volume-0 as "off"**: `isEffectivelyMuted()` (both modules) adds `volume <= 0`. Stat now means "app was producing audible signal at the solve" — OS/device mute remains invisible to web pages (no API; confirmed with user this is a platform limit, and trend comparisons stay valid).
- **Credits on CG**: dev-site URL now shows as PLAIN TEXT (`official-intelligence.art`, new `.cg-only` inverse utility + `.credits-url`) — CG's community-links exception explicitly allows dev-site references; only the clickable anchor risked "leads to a playable web version". TANTЯO plug stays fully hidden (real cross-promo). Profanity censored on CG via inline cg-hide/cg-only twins: "Eat that sh*t" (pasta recipe — user had forgotten this one) + "little sh*t"/"f***ing" (Lindsay blurb). Grawlix judged sufficient for PEGI 12; noted to user that the blurb's THEMES out-spice its vocabulary but are a vanishingly small QA target buried in credits. "ass"/"damn" deliberately left (mild-language tier).
- Version bumped 0.74 → 0.75. All changes traced or arithmetic-verified; CG-only behaviors need on-CG eyeballing (ring clearance, mute pause/resume, censored credits).
- Ships everything in the two 2026-07-12 entries below (they carry the detail; both were "unreleased" until now): the 1-path size retune (`startDimsFor` — Zen 4×5, Marathon 5×5, PotD 1-2 path ~75% LOW tier), width-first `{c}×{r}` dims display, the lbTab-style mode selector, the Practice→Zen rename, the "📅 Daily & More" first-run button, the first-solve PotD nudge (tooltip action-button support), and Zen as the first mode tab.
- Reminder from the retune: PotD's new LOW-tier sizes only apply at the NEXT daily seed (slots generate once/day); a dev PotD reset re-seeds today.
- Version bumped 0.73 → 0.74. Sizing/labels traced through code; mode-tab strip + menu fit previously measured live on production via injected CSS.
- **"Practice" renamed to "🧘 Zen"** (display only — the internal `'practice'` key is untouched everywhere: mode-picker storage, tracking funnel labels, config tables, so no migration and stats stay continuous). All 15 `mode.practice.name` strings + the static markup fallback; localized as 禅/젠/Дзен/زِن/ज़ेन where "Zen" isn't the loanword. Alternatives considered: Free Play (clearer, blander), Casual. `mode.practice.desc` still does the explaining, unchanged.
- **First-run quit button now reads "📅 Daily & More"** (was "More Modes"): the user's concern was that auto-started first-timers never learn PotD/streaks exist — this makes the one persistent button they see advertise the daily.
- **PotD nudge**: one-time tooltip fires as puzzle 2 of the first-visit auto-start run appears (right after the FIRST solve — pitching the daily at the moment the player is provably hooked, not at load). Its action button jumps straight into today's s1 daily via the same path a menu card click takes (quitToMenu → ModePicker.setMode('potd') → Potd.startPuzzle('s1')). tooltip.js gained generic optional-action support: `showOnce(key, msg, {label, onClick})`, new `#tooltipActionBtn` (CTA-weight gold fill) in a `.tooltipBtnRow`; click order is mark-seen → dismiss → onClick so the action can tear down game state without racing the card. Plain tips are unaffected (button stays hidden).
- **Zen is now the FIRST mode tab** on the menu (Zen · PotD · Marathon) — default mode's gold underline sits where reading starts. Order is display-only; all wiring keys off `data-mode`.
- If the nudge is dismissed via navigation (not Got-it/action), it never refires — deliberate: level-2-of-first-run happens once ever, and by then the player is on the menu where PotD is visible anyway.
- **1-path singular starts retuned** via new `MARATHON.startDimsFor(quad, paths, practice)` → `{rows, cols}` (supports asymmetric starts): Practice 4 tall × 5 WIDE (was 4×4), Marathon 5×5 (was the flat 8×8). Consumed by marathon.js `dimsForLevel` (growth still adds per-axis on top) and game.js `STARTER_PLAN` (s1 pre-gen now builds 5×5 so Marathon's first puzzle stays cache-instant). `MIN_DIM_PRACTICE_SINGULAR[0]` is now a dead fallback — comment in config.js says so.
- **PotD 1-2 path slots run a ~75% LOW size tier** (potd.js `SIZE_*_LOW`): singular axes [6,9] avg [7,9]; quad even axes [10,14] avg [11,14] (floors reject only the min×min roll, mirroring the full tier). 3-4 path slots unchanged. Sizes apply at the NEXT daily seed — slots generate once/day (first player generates, server stores); a dev PotD reset re-seeds today at the new sizes.
- **HUD dims display is now width-first**: all 15 `marathon.hudLevel` templates flipped `{r}×{c}` → `{c}×{r}` (placeholders keep their rows/cols meaning; only template order changed). Practice's first puzzle reads "Puzzle 1 · 5×4".
- **Menu mode selector restyled to the leaderboard's `.lbTab` underline-tab language** (user judged the pill buttons too similar to the game-type cards to read as a selector): transparent flat tabs on a hairline strip, gold text + gold underline on `.selected`; ≤500px squeeze rules mirror `.lbTab`'s. mode-picker.js unchanged. Verified via CSS injection on production at 615px: strip 480×34, no label clipping, selection + subtitle tracking correct, menu fits with 0 overflow (strip is shorter than the pills were).
- **Pitfall (session workflow)**: browser-pane verification against production left menu music playing through the user's speakers (pane audio is audible even when the pane isn't visible). Close the pane (preview_stop with the serverId from preview_list — NOT the session previewId) or mute the tab after testing audio-bearing pages.
- **v0.72's settings compression never applied on CG** — the rules were correct but sat inside the menu's moderate-squeeze media block (~line 1240), BEFORE the base `#settingsPopup` rules (~1398). Media queries add no priority; equal specificity → source order → base won every property. The v0.72 verification missed it because the test rules were injected as an appended `<style>` with `!important` (wins regardless of position) — the VALUES were validated, the CASCADE wasn't.
- Fix: the settings squeeze now lives in its own `@media` block placed AFTER the settings base section (search "Settings popup squeeze"), with a placement-matters comment + a pointer note left in the old location. Order audited: base → new block → ≤500px block (mutually exclusive range).
- **Lesson for future CSS verification: simulate the cascade honestly** — appended `<style>` WITHOUT `!important` loses/wins ties exactly like a same-file rule at that position. Re-verified that way on production: 684px → popup 585px, clamps computing (gap 11.8px vs base 16), no scrollbar, 50px clearance; 615px → 556px, fits.
- Version bumped 0.72 → 0.73. Only `styles.css` changed (+ version pair). If CG STILL shows a scrollbar after this deploy, suspect a stale build in their preview (hard-refresh) — the cascade is verified end-to-end.
- **Settings popup now FITS 501-920px viewports** (v0.71's 94vh cap+scrollbar demoted to last-resort backstop). New vh-fluid rules in the moderate squeeze block: popup padding/gap, h2, .settingsRow padding, label/select fonts, Close button — every clamp coefficient resolves to its base value at ~920px (no jump crossing the breakpoint; measured 9px on a 708px popup). Verified by injecting the rules into production: no scrollbar at 540/615/680px; 501-540px sliver would still scroll via the cap if content ever grows.
- **Now Playing panel scales with vmin** (was vw-clamped → full-size in CG's wide-but-short ~680px iframe, the exact place space is scarce): font/padding/gaps/offsets/max-width all vmin-fluid. Measured on production: 320×81 → 177×58 at 680px tall; full size restored at ≥~1000px (max-width now caps ~26vmin, so desktop is ~280px vs the old 320px — titles ellipsize slightly sooner). **Panel is now click-transparent except the ⏮⏯⏭ row** so big grids stay playable underneath; deliberate trade-off: a click on the song title reaches the tile under it. Declined-for-now option: auto-collapse to a ♪ pill a few seconds after each song change (needs JS in the song-change handler) — revisit if visual overlap still annoys.
- **Solve-audio stats respect the CG container mute**: new `Music.isEffectivelyMuted()` / `Sfx.isEffectivelyMuted()` (= player setting OR platform externalMuted); both recordSolve call sites (marathon + potd onSolve) switched to it. `isMuted()` deliberately unchanged (Settings UI / Now Playing / credits-blurb still mean "player's own setting"). Off-CG identical to before (externalMuted never set there); no back-end change (same booleans, truer values). Known nuance: volume slider at 0% still counts as "on" — pre-existing, left as-is.
- Version bumped 0.71 → 0.72. Settings + Now Playing sizing measured live on production via injected CSS; stats change traced (CG-only behavior). Files changed vs v0.71: `styles.css`, `music.js`, `audio.js`, `marathon.js`, `potd.js` (+ version pair).
- **User's v0.70 CG test**: auto-start worked, but music didn't play until the Settings button was clicked, and the Settings popup overflowed the CG iframe (title + CLOSE both clipped, unreachable — overlay centers it so it clips BOTH ends).
- **Root cause, music**: right-click = CW rotate (the natural first input) fires `contextmenu`, which was in NONE of the gesture listener sets — music.js gestureReady, audio.js WebAudio unlock, intro.js kickMusic all listened click/touchend/keydown only. A right-click-only player generated zero recognized gestures; the Settings click was the first real 'click', which is why music started exactly then. Added 'contextmenu' to all three sets (canvas's contextmenu handler preventDefaults the browser menu but does NOT stopPropagation, so it reaches document; capture-phase unlock listeners still run before the bubble-phase kick).
- **kickMusic now RE-ARMS** until a song actually engages (`getCurrentSong()` truthy) or music is muted. Previously it self-removed on first fire — if the umbrella API (Render, can cold-start ~30s+) hadn't delivered the song lists yet, that first start() just recorded shouldPlay and music was stranded until something else poked start() (e.g. a rejected-play recovery via start()'s resume branch, which keys off the ELEMENT's paused state — see v0.65-era notes).
- **Settings popup**: base `#settingsPopup` gained `max-height: 94vh; overflow-y: auto` (natural height ~44rem > CG's ~615-680px iframe). The ≤500px squeeze block keeps its own tighter 97vh + compressed-spacing override.
- Version bumped 0.70 → 0.71. Not browser-verified — CG-path behaviors only reproduce on crazygames.com origins (pane screenshots also broken this session); event flow traced through code. **Retest on CG: fresh profile, make the FIRST action a right-click on a tile — expect the rotate click SFX AND music to start together.**
- Files changed vs v0.70: `music.js`, `audio.js`, `intro.js`, `styles.css` (+ the version pair).

## 2026-07-11 — Release v0.70 (CG conversion funnel + SDK + censored songs)
- **Context: CG Basic Launch stats** (123 plays): playtime 5:56 (decent), conversion 55% (CG wants 80%+), D1 3.08% (noise-level sample), CTR 0.7% (~1% is platform avg). Diagnosis: game holds players once playing; the funnel before/after leaks. This release attacks conversion; the new cover art (below) attacks CTR.
- **First-visit auto-start** (ALL platforms, not just CG): brand-new visitors skip the menu → straight into a 1-path Practice puzzle (4×4). `Marathon.autoStartFirstPractice()` called from intro.js's dismiss AND its CG auto-skip bail. "New" = no `_firstVisitAutoStarted_v1` flag AND no `_mode`/`_lastPlayerName` (protects pre-feature returning players). Flag consumed BEFORE startGame so a failure can't loop. localStorage-unavailable (private mode) → normal menu. Verified live on production end-to-end.
- **During that one run only**: HUD gets a How-to-Solve button (center slot — free because Practice hides #hudTimer) and Quit is relabeled "More Modes" (new `marathon.moreModes` key ×15 langs). Both restored in goToMenu (covers PotD's showMenu path which bypasses startGame). Tutorial is now safe to open MID-GAME: new `Tutorial.isOpen()` + game.js undo() gate (Ctrl+Z was the one input the modal can't block — it would have popped real-run undoStack entries against the swapped-in tutorial grid).
- **Menu: inline 3-mode segmented selector** replaced the chip+popup (players never discovered the popup). mode-picker.js rewritten, same public API; modal markup + `.selection-*` CSS + `#modeBtn` pulse all removed. **Footer-clip fix**: the ≤500px squeeze block predates the v0.63 footer — base 1.4rem margin-top pushed it off ~430px windows (18px overflow, measured live via injected CSS on production). Mirrored the moderate block's clamp; 430px now exact fit, ~390px still scrolls ~21px (pre-existing, scroll fallback reachable). CG's 615px iframe unaffected.
- **Edge-cross filler ban, SINGULAR ONLY** (maze.js pickFillerType now takes r,c): a cross is rotation-invariant, so an edge filler cross could never be valid — a free "it's filler" tell. Quad mode deliberately EXEMPT: quads scramble sub-tile POSITIONS, so even solution crosses legitimately visit boundary cells mid-solve; banning filler ones there would INVERT the leak (edge cross ⇒ "mis-rotated solution quad"). Solution crosses can't reach singular boundary except entry/exit notch cells (visibly connected).
- **Menu no longer shows the finished puzzle behind it**: Maze.clear()+Render.draw() in marathon goToMenu AND potd showMenu (separate return path). Body background image untouched (lives on documentElement, not canvas). game.js undo() bails on null grid — otherwise menu-time Ctrl+Z would loadSnapshot the board right back.
- **Censored songs** (music.js): row keyed `<base>_censored` = clean re-cut of `<base>`. On CG, explicit song with a twin is REPLACED (twin's label/filename at the ORIGINAL's list position); twins never play standalone anywhere; orphan twins (no base in any list) play as normal songs. Twin lookup spans the union of all 4 lists. Twin must be attached to the game + ≥1 list, EXPLICIT unchecked. All pre-cache-write, so offline pools stay correct.
- **CrazyGames SDK v3** (new `cg-sdk.js`, loads after config.js): pure no-op off-CG (zero external requests). Loading events (loadingStart at init / loadingStop at window load), gameplayStart (marathon onPuzzleReady + PotD play-start; replay-proof) / gameplayStop (goToMenu, gameOver, potd showMenu), tutorial open/close = pause/resume, happytime() only on top-20 rank. Async-init reconcile: pre-init calls tracked as state and replayed in the right order (loading before first gameplayStart — that's their load-time stamp).
- **Container mute** (CHECK the "supports CrazyGames muting audio through SDK" box on the submission form): non-persisted `externalMuted` layer in music.js (volume-0 override, playback state machines untouched) + audio.js (gates all 4 play entries, stops loops, fades one-shots). Structurally outranks in-game toggles (every output path routes through it) per CG's priority rule; player's saved settings never written. Wired from cg-sdk.js settings + addSettingsChangeListener.
- **Cover art**: cover-art.html gained STYLE: BOLD (default) — big tile-O ring hero with 4 thick routes plugging into its ports, wordmark below, NO tagline (CG rejects text beyond the game title — CLASSIC's "TWIST·CONNECT·SOLVE" is a CG rejection risk; CLASSIC kept for itch/social). All 3 CG-required sizes (1920×1080 / 800×1200 / 800×800). Not deployed — download PNGs and upload on the CG portal. Preview artifact: https://claude.ai/code/artifact/b85016c6-c968-4a2d-b44f-ac3fca9d2242
- **Deploy facts learned**: the site deploys via a separate git repo → Netlify (the zip is ITCH-ONLY). Mid-session incident: a file drop into the repo only carried maze.js (watch Explorer's Replace-or-Skip dialog). `cg-sdk.js` is a NEW file — must be git-ADDED, not just overwritten. CG SDK behavior only testable on CG origins (their preview env).
- Version bumped 0.69 → 0.70. Auto-start funnel verified live on production (fresh-profile pane: I-agree → practice puzzle, How-to visible, "More Modes" label, flag consumed); the rest traced through code paths — dev-server preview skipped per standing AV preference.
- **New `IS_CRAZYGAMES` flag** (config.js, hostname ends `crazygames.com`; worker-safe) + `html.is-crazygames` class. Gates everything CG-specific in the one codebase: intro auto-skips (their ≤1-click-to-gameplay rule — menu cards ARE the one click; a big PLAY button was added then removed as redundant), fullscreen toggle + F11/PageUp/PageDown never register (custom fullscreen controls prohibited; they only ever attach inside intro's init, so the intro bail covers it), share rows/`#shareOverlay`/TANTЯO + official-intelligence.art credits links hidden (`.cg-hide` + CSS; no links to playable versions elsewhere), SW registration skipped (update-flow reload could flash during their QA). On intro auto-skip, menu music starts via one-time bubble-phase click/keydown/touchend listeners (fires AFTER music.js's capture-phase gesture blessing — Music.start() pre-gesture is a no-op that doesn't queue).
- **Menu now fits short embed iframes** (CG hands a ~615px desktop iframe). Root causes: (1) `justify-content: center` + `overflow:auto` pushed overflow into an unreachable scroll region — replaced with flex-start + auto-margin pair (titleFrame margin-top / legal-footer margin-bottom): centers when content fits, top-anchors + scrolls when not. (2) The moderate squeeze block was `pointer: coarse`-only — now also `(max-height:920px) and (min-height:501px)` any-pointer, with vh-fluid clamps (lower floors only bind under ~733px, so iPads resolve to the same sizes as before — that sizing was user-tuned, don't re-shrink it). How-to button joined the bottom-row shrink rule (needs the `.menuBottomBtns` prefix — its base #id rule is later in the file).
- **platform-redirect.js is itch-ONLY now**: redirect action gated on itch host/referrer (`isItchEmbed()`); unknown embedders inert. Previously ANY foreign iframe triggered it — on CG mobile it would have hijacked their page top-level = instant QA rejection. Tracking's `embedded` flag keeps the broad any-embed meaning.
- **CORS for CG** (deploys outside this zip): umbrella API app.py got `games.crazygames.com` + a `^https://[a-z0-9.-]+\.crazygames\.com$` regex entry (flask-cors treats regex-metachar strings as patterns); circuitousness-api got the origins via its `CORS_ORIGINS` env var (user-edited on Render). Real preview origin is per-game: `circuitousness.game-files.crazygames.com` — the console CORS error names the origin when in doubt.
- **Explicit-songs system** (PEGI 12; changed mind from v0.68's "risk it"): umbrella API `Song.explicit` boolean + one-time boot migration that backfills 33 keys from a full lyric review of both games' `Music/Lyrics` folders (backfill ONLY runs when the column is first created — admin edits never clobbered). Admin songs page got an EXPLICIT checkbox + 🔞 marker. music.js filters `explicit` rows from all 4 pools on CG BEFORE mapAndSort + the cache write. `my_bitches` deliberately NOT flagged (it's about dogs, per user); cleaned rewrites (Teeeerry Redux Redux ×2, Show Tunes, Meet Cuter/Cutest) + the Colbert INSTRUMENTAL not flagged; borderline-but-clean calls logged in session: Get Some Tonight, Blobs (Get Me High), Flood Me, Spontaneous Human Consumption, The Pit (Horny Mix). TANTЯO's music.js does NOT have the filter yet — port it if TANTЯO ever goes on CG.
- **Requires before CG retest**: push official-intelligence-api (CORS regex + explicit migration) and official-intelligence-web (admin checkbox). CG QA also still expects their HTML5 SDK integrated before publishing — not done yet. Marketing-creatives folder (optional submission field) also not built.

Both callers' `state !== PLAYING` guards exclude replays. New `Sfx.isMuted()` getter in audio.js (Music already had one); missing module/getter reports "off". Per-PUZZLE counters, not sticky per-visit booleans — the stat is "% of solves with music on".

Also this session (post-v0.66-release, same dashboard file): Return Players Audit now defaults to TODAY (CT) instead of yesterday, and the LOAD button was replaced by ◀ ▶ day-step arrows (`stepReturning(delta)`, UTC-noon date math so DST can't skip a day).

## 2026-07-01 — Release v0.65
- Leaderboard replay: the "Now Playing" box now only shows if the ORIGINAL player had music on. A music-off recording drives no scripted playback, so the watcher's own (silently-continuing) menu music was leaking into the box. Fixed by splitting "replay in progress" from "recording had music" in `music.js`: new `replayActive` (bracketed by the replay driver via `Music.setReplayActive`, held across the whole session so it survives inter-puzzle gaps where `scriptedPlayback` goes null) + `replayHadMusic` (flips true only when scripted songs actually schedule). Now Playing visibility = `replayActive ? didReplayHaveMusic() : !isMuted()`. `setReplayActive(true)` fires `notifySongChange()` — that was the missing signal that lets a music-off replay hide the box on start (startScriptedPlayback no-ops silently for empty events).
- `game.js` `replay()` and `replayAll()` now call `Music.setReplayActive(true/false)` around the whole replay (session-level, not per-puzzle, to avoid box flicker between marathon puzzles). Guarded by `typeof Music` like the existing scripted-playback calls; matches existing no-try/finally style (same fragility as the pre-existing `isReplaying` handling).
- Preserved prior intent: during a music-ON replay the box still shows the recorded title even if the WATCHER has music muted (keys off the recording, not the watcher's mute).
- Version bumped 0.64 → 0.65. Not browser-verified — repro needs an actual music-off leaderboard recording; dev-server preview declined this session (AV).

Implemented as a decorative `<span class="btnIcon" aria-hidden="true">🏆</span>` sibling, with `data-i18n="marathon.leaderboards"` moved onto an inner `<span>`. Reason: `marathon.leaderboards` is shared with the leaderboard panel's `<h2>` heading, so baking the emoji into the i18n string (the "How to Solve" `❓` pattern) would have put a trophy on the heading too and required editing 15 language blocks. The DOM helper does `el.innerHTML = text` on the `data-i18n` element, so an icon sibling survives translation. No `.btnIcon` CSS yet — emoji renders inline; class is a future styling hook.

## 2026-06-24 — Release v0.60
- **Practice is now the default mode** for new players (`mode-picker.js` `DEFAULT_MODE` 'potd' → 'practice'). Existing players keep their saved `_mode`; only fresh profiles land in Practice.
- **Practice singular puzzles start smaller, scaled by path count** (was a flat 8×8). New `MARATHON.MIN_DIM_PRACTICE_SINGULAR = [4, 6, 8]` table in config.js (indexed by pathCount-1 → 1-path 4×4, 2-path 6×6, 3-path 8×8). 4-path is NOT in the table — it keeps the generator-mandated `MIN_DIM_SINGULAR_4PATH` (10×10) floor regardless of mode, since the strict 4-path generator needs the room for 8 endpoints. `minDimFor(quadMode, pathCount, practice)` gained a 3rd `practice` arg, and `marathon.js` `dimsForLevel` passes the module-level `isPractice`. Quad is untouched (already starts at 4). Marathon stays 8×8. Pre-gen STARTER_PLAN is unchanged (still 8×8), so the FIRST practice puzzle misses cache and shows a near-instant build banner; lookahead (`upcomingDims`→`dimsForLevel`) pre-gens the right practice sizes from then on.
- **Fixed stale "Puzzle N · 8×8" label on PotD.** Root cause: `#hudLevel:empty{display:none}` in styles.css is the design (PotD is meant to leave it empty), but PotD never cleared the leftover Marathon/Practice text. `potd.js` now grabs `#hudLevel` in init and clears it in `startPuzzle`'s HUD setup. No i18n change needed.
- **Leaderboard default tab follows the new default mode.** `showLeaderboard` mapped Practice players to the Marathon board ("closest equivalent"); now Practice (which has no board of its own) defaults to the Puzzle of the Day board. Only Marathon-mode players default to Marathon: `initialMode = picked === 'marathon' ? 'marathon' : 'potd'`. Since Practice is now the default mode, a fresh player checking the leaderboard lands on the PotD board.
- **Practice solves now fire `Tracking.recordFinish()`** (marathon.js `onSolve`, gated `isPractice && solvedCount === 1` right after `solvedCount++`). Practice never reaches `gameOver()` (untimed, ends only on Quit), which is the sole place Marathon fires the funnel "finished" milestone — so without this, the now-default Practice mode showed all play as "started, never finished". Gated to the FIRST solve per run: user only cares that a visitor finished *a* puzzle (not how many), and the server's `/finished` flag is a sticky per-visit boolean, so first-solve firing records exactly that signal without re-PATCHing every subsequent solve. **No back-end change** — per-visit sticky boolean is the desired semantics; explicitly decided NOT to add a per-solve counter.
- Not browser-verified — user declined the dev server (AV/EDR killed the session yesterday on bulk file activity). Changes traced through the code paths instead.

## 2026-06-24 — Release v0.58

Front-end-only change; needs the circuitousness-api redeploy to store the new fields and the OI-web push for the dashboard panel.

## 2026-06-24 — Embed/redirect visit tagging (signal source)
- WHY: admin "Recent Visits" showed many mobile ○○○ "bailed at intro" rows that are NOT rejections — on mobile+itch-embed, `platform-redirect.js` throws its "play the full version" overlay OVER the intro, so the player never reaches the menu on the itch instance. Needed to tell handoffs apart from real intro bounces.
- `platform-redirect.js` now exposes `window.__platformRedirect = { embedded, fired }` (set before its early-return): `embedded` = any itch-embed load incl. desktop; `fired` = it actually diverted a mobile player. `tracking.js` reads that via `_loadContext()` and sends `embedded` + `redirected` on `/api/visit`.
- Pitfall: the back-end's 5-min same-session dedup reuses the first row, so embedded/redirected reflect the FIRST load (consistent with referrer-first-wins); fine.
- NOT done: per-user correlation id to stitch a specific itch bounce to its real-origin continuation (different origins → different session_ids, so they can't be linked yet). Deferred — aggregate on-site funnel answers "is the intro the problem" without it.

## 2026-06-23 — Release v0.57
- **PotD puzzle sizing: raised floor + added avg floor** (potd.js generateDims). Was singular axes [6,12] capped only by avg ceiling ≤9 → 6×6 easy puzzles were in-spec. Now singular axes [8,12], avg window [9,12]; quad even sub-tile axes [12,18] (6–9 quad-tiles), avg [14,18]. New `SIZE_AVG_MIN`/`_QUAD` floor stops small-on-both-axes rolls. Also rewrote the 3 stale comment blocks that described larger ranges the code never matched. Monte-Carlo verified (no 6×6, ~7–8 worst-case re-rolls). NOTE: PotD puzzles are seeded once/day server-side, so new sizes apply to slots seeded AFTER deploy (next day, or a dev PotD reset to re-seed today).

Music + SFX stay visible so phone players can set audio prefs BEFORE the "I agree" tap starts music (the real gap). Removed the old whole-row `display:none` in `showFullscreenHint`. Verified via preview: desktop = all 3 toggles; mobile path = Music/SFX `flex`, Full Screen `none`.

## 2026-06-23 — Release v0.55
- **`agreed` funnel milestone (live experiment)**: tracking.js gains `recordAgreed()`, fired from intro.js `dismiss()` when the player clears the warning/"I agree" intro and reaches the mode menu. Purpose: split no-start visits into "bailed at the warning" vs "reached the menu, never picked a card" — diagnosing whether the intro gate (vs Tantro's instant-start button) is a real barrier to entry.

Admin dashboard shows a "Reached menu" card + "Warning→Menu / Menu→Start" rates.

## 2026-06-22 — Pitfall: Claude Preview tool's `vh` ≠ emulated viewport
- When verifying responsive CSS in the Claude Preview tool, `vh` units compute against the tool's REAL window height, not the `preview_resize` emulated viewport — so vh-based sizes render at the wrong scale in the preview even though `matchMedia`/`innerHeight` correctly report the emulated size. Bit me during the How-to-Solve tutorial + short-landscape menu work (v0.50–0.53): a `clamp(…, 16vh, …)` thumbnail measured 62px at an emulated 390px-tall viewport because 16vh resolved against the ~657px real window.
- Workaround: for short-viewport rules that MUST be verified in-tool, use fixed `rem` (predictable in both preview and real device) — that's why the `@media (max-height:500px)` menu thumbnail is a flat `3.2rem`, not vh-based. For vh-based rules, don't trust the preview's rendered px; reason about real-device vh manually or check on a real phone.

## 2026-06-22 — Release v0.53
- **Share-tracking anti-inflation.** Accidental taps on the score-popup share icons were inflating shares. (1) New source flag: shares from the "Enjoying it?" popup (`#shareOverlay`) record immediately as `kind:'popup'`; legacy shares stay uncategorized. (2) Score-popup shares (game-over + "Solved!" rows) are now DEFERRED — not recorded at click time; reported only on the player's next interaction (`pointerdown`/`keydown`), binned by delay: `deliberate` if ≥ `SHARE_INTENT_THRESHOLD_MS` (15s, config.js) else `quick`; no further interaction → never recorded. Copy-link uses the same timing logic. `share.js` holds one `pendingShare`; `tracking.js` `recordShare(platform, kind)` sends `kind` in the `/shared` PATCH. Verified in-browser via a `Tracking.recordShare` spy (popup→immediate, score→deferred, no-interaction→none, <15s→quick, ≥15s→deliberate).
- **Back-end (needs Render deploy):** `page_visits` gains 3 sticky booleans `share_from_popup` / `share_score_quick` / `share_score_deliberate` (idempotent ADD COLUMN migrations, existing rows backfill FALSE); `record_share` sets them from `kind`; `/api/admin/visits/stats` returns the new counts (lifetime + 24h/7d/30d). Admin dashboard (separate `official-intelligence-web` repo) updated to display the breakdown.

## 2026-06-22 — Release v0.50
- **New "How to Solve" interactive tutorial** (new `tutorial.js` + render/menu/i18n/tooltip changes). Button on the main menu (left of Leaderboards, matched height via `.menuBottomBtns` flex row). Modal plays a hand-authored 5×6 fixed puzzle through 8 teaching beats, one per Next-click, with animated tile rotations + a DOM cursor. Drives the REAL Maze/Render/Gates via a new persistent "tutorial render mode" in render.js (`beginTutorial`/`endTutorial`/`tutorialMetrics` — a sticky variant of `renderSnippet` that retargets the live renderer to the modal canvas and renders normally). Puzzle design hits all beats along one path: elbow start → lock → twin unison → twin lock → the straight/elbow-twin trap (exploits the straight's 180° symmetry; locking at the look-right delta leaves the elbow twin 180° wrong) → unlock+fix → two-gate "clear one, block an earlier, then clear both" sequence (shared `Gates.delta`, vertices 4,3 & 4,5) → gold completion. ~half the straights start horizontal incl. pre-aligned path tiles that light "for free". Reaching step 8 sets `<slug>_tutorialWatched`, which tooltip.js reads to suppress the `lockTile` + `twinStraightLock` gameplay tips.
- **Tutorial modal sizing**: all 8 bullets pre-built (hidden) so the modal is full-height from the start and never resizes/scrolls as steps reveal (fade-only, no slide). vh-based clamps + canvas `max-height` so it fits short landscape phones without clipping or scrollbars.
- **Menu fix for short landscape phones**: the existing `@media (max-height:500px)` block shrank only the old single Leaderboards button; with the new 2-button stretch row that left How-to at full size, inflating the row off-screen. Now both bottom buttons shrink together + thumbnails reduced to fixed 3.2rem.
- i18n: 16 new `tutorial.*` keys across all 15 languages. No back-end changes.

## 2026-06-18 — Release v0.47
- **Hardened the itch.io mobile redirect** (`platform-redirect.js`) after an itch comment ("Doesn't work on phone?" / direct link worked on Galaxy A53). Root cause: PWA features + the seamless top-nav redirect can't work inside itch's sandboxed iframe, so mobile itch players were left with only a fragile new-tab button. Overlay now has TWO tiered, guaranteed actions: PRIMARY anchor `target="_blank"` → new top-level tab (installable PWA, works under itch's allow-popups); SECONDARY plain same-frame anchor → navigates the iframe's own context (never sandbox-blocked) so the real game always loads in-frame (playable, not installable). Two distinct anchors, no scripted window.open → no popup-blocker fragility, no double-open.
- Copy rewritten action-first ("Play the full version" / "On phones, the game runs best on its own site…") since "it works at all" beats "it installs." New `redirect.fallback` i18n key + updated title/body/button across all 15 languages.
- Still inert on desktop, in standalone PWA, and on our own origin. Couldn't reproduce itch's sandbox locally (overlay correctly inert on localhost) — real test is uploading the zip and opening the itch listing on a phone.

## 2026-06-17 — Release v0.46
- **New "Practice" mode** (🧩) — third option in the MODE picker, alongside PotD + Marathon. Plays exactly like Marathon but **untimed** and **never saved**. Implemented as an `isPractice` flag inside `marathon.js` (reuses the whole state machine): `onPuzzleReady` skips `startTimer()` so there's no countdown and the time-expiry `gameOver()` path is never reached (→ no leaderboard save screen); the game only ends on Quit. `onHintUsed` returns early (free hints, no penalty, marathonHint tip suppressed). Solve-transition subline shows `marathon.solvePractice` instead of banked time. No session token requested; tracking reports mode `'practice'`. Menu Leaderboards button routes Practice players to the Marathon board (Practice has no board). Timer hidden via `body.mode-practice #hudTimer { display:none }`. `mode.practice.name`/`mode.practice.desc`/`marathon.solvePractice` added to all 15 langs.
- Verified in-browser: Practice HUD has no timer + never counts; HINT applies no penalty; Marathon timer still counts (no regression); no console errors.

- **Default tile face opacity raised 0.69 → 0.80** (settings.js `DEF_TILE_FACE`). Fresh installs only — `loadFloat` keeps existing players' persisted values. Twins now default to 0.88 (0.80×1.1). render.js `TILE_FACE_ALPHA` init was already 0.80 so they match.

State still restores via snapshot; new `animateUndoMove(move)` plays the reverse spin as a visual layer — CW rotate undoes CCW, hint's CW `turns` reverse CCW, gate spin reverses (twins handled inside `animateRotationAt`, locks have no spin). Undo stacks (live + replay) now store `{state, move}`; hint moves record `turns` so replay undos animate too. Verified via spying on `animateRotationAt`: rotate/hint/replay all fire the correct inverse call, state restored, no errors.

## 2026-06-17 — Release v0.43
- **UNDO feature** (game.js + index.html + styles.css + i18n): new HUD button left of the timer, mirroring HINT's offset (`#hudUndoBtn` at `right: calc(50% + 10rem)`; `#hudLevel` moved to far-left to free the slot); debug-mode `#undoBtn` stacked under HINT (top-right, clear of the slider panel). Cyan accent, disabled when nothing to undo, Ctrl/⌘+Z shortcut. Snapshot-based, unlimited (capped `MAX_UNDO_DEPTH=200`, resets per puzzle via startRecording). Undo is RECORDED as an `{type:'undo'}` replayable move (via new `appendMove`) — playback reflects the undos themselves; `playOneRecording` mirrors a replay-side undo stack (`applyReplayUndo`). `undo.button` i18n added to all 15 langs.

Crowded hues separated by LIGHTNESS. Sequenced greedy max-min so low-pair puzzles get the most-different colors first.

## 2026-06-10 — Release v0.40

- **Sealed-ring quad fix** (maze.js `breakSymmetricFillerQuads`): all-filler quads of 4 inward-facing elbows formed a closed loop with no external ports — invisible to fixDeadEnds (all inner ports match) and skipped by every straight-lane pattern (patternSafe always fails on the ring). Now detected via "all-elbow + all-filler + zero valid patterns" and fully rewritten (straight lane on a random pair + outward elbows on the rest). ## 2026-05-26 — Release v0.26

- **HUGE batch of fixes accumulated since v0.25 — coming out together because user was testing iteratively without a /rel.** Don't expect future releases to be this dense.
- **audience_awww SFX** (audio.js + game.js): new 6-variant SFX type, fires when `Maze.pathsWon[i]` transitions true→false in `refresh()` (completed path broken, any cause). Triggers only on FULLY-COMPLETED path breaks; partial-chain breaks stay with the existing twin-twist OR-pick (fail | disappointed | gasp). Cinematic_bass also now fires at PotD startPuzzle (mirroring Marathon's onPuzzleReady cue).
- **Hint port-equivalent fix** (maze.js): hint()'s Pass 3 random-fallback was picking tiles whose CURRENT rotation already gave the same port pairs as their solution rotation (CROSS at any rotation, STRAIGHT at the sibling-rotation pair). applyHintAt would rotate them anyway, producing a confusing 180° spin that conveyed nothing because the path through the tile was unchanged. Now Pass 3 filters port-equivalent tiles entirely via `rotationsHaveSamePorts(t.type, t.rotation, t._solution)` — every hint actually rotates something meaningful. applyHintAt also has a port-equivalent skip as defense-in-depth (replay of older recordings, etc.). Edge case: nearly-solved puzzles where every remaining eligible tile is port-equivalent will get a null hint (user accepted this).
- **Twin-twist SFX asymmetry fix** (game.js handlePointer): the OR-pick (fail | disappointed | gasp) had an `if (aInLost) continue;` filter that skipped firing whenever the rotated tile A's own cell dropped from highlighted — meant to suppress "player broke their own simple path" cases but over-aggressive. User reported CW twin twist firing SFX but CCW not, because CCW happened to drop A's cell while CW kept it lit. The remaining bOldLaneGone + remainderUnits>=2 checks already establish B is meaningfully contributing — `aInLost` filter removed.
- **Audience reactions Settings toggle** (settings.js + audio.js + index.html + i18n.js): new sub-mute that gates ONLY audience_* / applause / applause_long SFX. Mechanical cues (cinematic_bass, fail, glitch, jump_scare) unaffected. Row is grayed out when the parent SFX toggle is muted. 15-language i18n: settings.audienceReactions.
- **Music + replay polish** (music.js + credits.js): mute now respected during scripted playback (playSong gates on `muted`). Now Playing panel shows song title during replay with controls hidden. Shuffle bag + menu bag persist to localStorage (mirrors credits bag + TANTЯO). Credits scroll time-based instead of frame-based (30px/sec wall-clock; iPad Safari's rAF throttling no longer drops speed), rendered via translate3d for smooth sub-pixel motion.
- **PotD changes**: hints policy reversed — no longer disqualifies, hints_used is primary leaderboard tiebreaker ahead of time_ms. Front-end sends hintsUsed in submit, server stores + sorts. Confirmation modal before hint use removed (matches Marathon's no-prompt behavior, tooltip educates instead). hint counter shows in PotD leaderboard rows. Tooltip + modal i18n updated across 15 languages: "will hurt your rank" instead of "will disqualify".
- **PotD timeouts** (potd.js): short-first / long-retry pattern. `FETCH_TIMEOUTS_MS = [4000, 12000]` (first attempt fails fast for transient stalls, retry has room for genuinely-slow responses). Same shape for SEED_TIMEOUTS_MS. Total ~16.5s budget — same as before but distributed for better UX.
- **iPad Safari black bar at bottom** (PWA fullscreen): meta theme-color and html bg-color changed from #0a0a2e to #000 to match TANTЯO. The system home-indicator strip iOS draws is tinted with theme-color (not manifest's value).
- **Email notifications** (back-end): TANTЯO-parity Gmail SMTP path on every Marathon submit (≥1 puzzle solved) and every eligible PotD submit. Requires GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD env var on Render — same secret as TANTЯO.

## 2026-05-26 — Release v0.25

- **PotD /today + /seed timeouts**: short-first / long-retry pattern (`[4000, 12000]` per-attempt array) replaces the prior single `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000` × 2-attempts setup. Same ~16.5s total budget but distributed so the first attempt fails fast on transient stalls (4s) while the retry has room for genuinely-slow responses (12s). Brief discussion with user about timeout strategy: short-timeout-+-retry wins for transient slowness (different worker / connection / pool slot on retry), short timeouts are neutral for sustained slowness (just fail faster, fall through to local-gen sooner). User saw a /today timeout where /seed worked seconds later — confirmed transient pattern, hence the redistribution.
- Earlier in this session I made the wrong direction first (8000→15000), then reverted after user pushed back. Worth remembering: when a fetch fails, "make the timeout longer" is the obvious move but often wrong on services like Render where transient slowness is more common than sustained. Per-attempt arrays > flat constants for retry strategy.

## 2026-05-25 — Release v0.24

- **Shuffle bag persistence** (music.js): the gameplay shuffle bag + the menu-phase bag now persist to localStorage (`<slug>_shuffleRemaining_v1` / `_shuffleFingerprint_v1`, same shape for menu). Previously the bags were in-memory only — every page reload reset them, so a player could hear a song they'd just played because the new bag's first pick was a fresh uniform random over the full pool. Mirrors TANTЯO's pattern (and Circuitousness's existing credits-bag persistence).
- **Bag refactor**: `queue`/`queuePos` collapsed into a single "remaining indices" array, `.shift()` to consume. Same shape as the existing `creditsBag`. Fingerprint includes pool IDs in order + `playMode` for shuffle (so a Settings mode change invalidates cleanly). Lazy-load on first pick, validate against current pool, fall back to fresh shuffle on any mismatch.
- **Behavior**: bag now picks up exactly where it left off after a reload — if you'd heard 3 of 10 songs before the reload, the next song is one of the remaining 7. Full N-song cycle completes before any repeats, across sessions.

## 2026-05-25 — Release v0.23

- **PotD hints policy reversed**: hint use no longer disqualifies — it's the primary leaderboard tiebreaker ahead of time_ms (fewer hints wins, same shape as Marathon's `solved DESC, hints ASC, time ASC` model). `potd.js` tracks `hintsUsed`, sends it in the submit payload; `noteHintUsed` increments instead of flipping `eligible`. The pre-hint confirmation modal in `game.js`'s handler is GONE — matches Marathon's no-prompt behavior; the tooltip educates instead. `tooltip.potdHint` and `potd.hintConfirm.body` updated across all 15 languages to "hurts your rank" wording.
- **Leaderboard shows hints for PotD too**: `paintLeaderboard`'s PotD branch previously emitted an empty padding span where the hints column lives — replaced with a real `lbHints` span using the same `marathon.lbHints` i18n key as the marathon branch. Legacy rows without a `hintsUsed` field default to 0. Back-end `PotdScore` gains a `hints_used` column + new composite index `(date, slot, hints_used, time_ms)` (migrations baked into `_COLUMN_MIGRATIONS`, including index DROP/CREATE) — requires Render redeploy.
- **Email notifications on completion**: TANTЯO-parity Gmail-SMTP path on the back-end. Every Marathon submit (which is gated by `solved >= 1` already) and every eligible PotD submit fires `send_score_notification('marathon'|'potd', ...)` after the rank query. Requires `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD` env var on Render — same secret already configured for TANTЯO works.

- **Blue bar at bottom in iPad PWA**: `<meta name="theme-color">` changed from `#0a0a2e` to `#000000` (matches TANTЯO's pattern) — iPadOS PWA fullscreen tints the home-indicator strip with the meta theme-color, NOT the manifest's. `html { background-color }` also `#000` now. Bar is still there, just black instead of blue. Open follow-up: dynamic theme-color matching the current bg-image's bottom edge color (see prior NOTES entry).

## 2026-05-25 — Open: dynamic theme-color for PWA bottom bar

- iPad PWA fullscreen mode (launching from home-screen icon, NOT regular Safari) draws a strip at the bottom for the home-indicator gesture area, tinted with the meta `theme-color`. `viewport-fit=cover` doesn't extend the page render INTO that strip — it's iOS-reserved chrome. v0.22 set the meta `theme-color` to `#000` (matching TANTЯO's pattern) so the bar is black against dark UI; against colorful background images (level1-50.jpg) the black bar is still visible but less obtrusive than the prior dark-navy.
- **Why TANTЯO doesn't show this**: TANTЯO's body is mostly black starfield; the same strip is there but invisible against its own background. Circuitousness's rotating colorful images make the bar always visible.
- **Potential follow-up the user didn't reject**: after each `applyBodyBackground()` image load (render.js), sample the image's bottom edge in an offscreen canvas, average to a single color, then `document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]').content = '#xxxxxx'`. Bar tracks each image's bottom color and effectively disappears into the image. Cost: ~ms canvas sample per image change. User said "if that's the best we can do" — they accepted static black for now but the dynamic option is a clean enhancement when convenient.

## 2026-05-25 — Release v0.22

- **iPad PWA fullscreen "blue bar at bottom" fix**: `<meta name="theme-color">` changed `#0a0a2e` → `#000000`, and `html { background-color }` from `#0a0a2e` → `#000`. Matches TANTЯO's exact pattern. iOS PWA fullscreen tints the home-indicator strip at the bottom using the HTML meta theme-color (NOT manifest's value), and the dark-navy default rendered as a visible dark-blue bar in PWA-launched mode (invisible in regular Safari). Manifest theme_color/background_color stay at `#0a0a2e` — those drive splash screen + Android Chrome UI which are fine with the dark navy. **Caveat**: existing PWA home-screen icons cache the manifest indefinitely; users may need to delete + re-add the icon to pick up future manifest changes, but this specific fix is via meta tag so it should propagate via the normal SW update.

## 2026-05-24 — Removed stage-2 audience cheer on PotD Save

- **potd.js `onSolve`**: stage-2 `audience_cheer` / `audience_cheer_long` (rank-dependent SFX after submit) is GONE. Was producing a "brief blast" on Save click: cheer fired at full volume, then within ~1 frame `quitToLeaderboard` ran `Sfx.fadeOneShots()` + `Music.start()` (which calls `playSong` → another `fadeOneShots`), clipping the cheer's onset into a startling truncated sound.
- **First attempt was wrong** — I relocated the cheer to AFTER `quitToLeaderboard` (so it'd survive the fade). User pushed back: NO SFX should play at Save-click time at all. Save is a quiet bookkeeping action; layering anything on it feels off. Stage-1 `applause_long` (line 1215, fires immediately on solve) is the celebration; rank is now communicated visually only (highlighted leaderboard row).
- **Don't re-add this** — if future work wants to celebrate rank, do it at a moment that's narratively "rank reveal," not glued to the Save button. Even then, beware `quitToLeaderboard`'s synchronous `fadeOneShots` + `Music.start` chain — any SFX fired in the same call stack will get clipped.

## 2026-05-24 — Release v0.20

- SW now bypasses cache on `localhost` / `127.0.0.1` so iterative dev doesn't require manual SW + Cache Storage clears every time. Production (real domain) unchanged.
- Pushes the day's accumulated fixes: PotD cross-browser reset detection (per-slot `generatedAt` reconcile), PotD name-entry dialog + shared iOS keyboard, PotD audience-cheer SFX + lingering-SFX fadeout on music transitions, Quad Marathon dim reduction (q1-3 → 4 logical, q4 → 6), hints tiebreaker on leaderboard sort (requires back-end deploy + `scores.hints_used` ALTER), 'Anonymous' default name, credits shuffle bag, marathon-replay music continuity, quad/singular mode-pollution fix, PotD checkmark refresh, PWA first-install no-reload, intro warning shuffle bag.

## 2026-05-24 — Music gate, hints tiebreaker, mode-pollution fix, PotD name entry

- **Music.start now gated on first user gesture** (music.js): module-level `gestureReady` flag flips on first click/touchend/keydown via capture-phase listeners on document. `start()` no-ops while `gestureReady === false`, AND doesn't set `shouldPlay = true` — so `refreshFromAPI`'s "auto-start once songs land" hook also skips. Localhost was hiding the bug because Chrome treats `localhost` permissively for autoplay (and Media Engagement Index covers frequent visitors); fresh production visitors would have hit "play() rejected, silent until intro Continue clicked." Boot-time `Marathon.init → goToMenu → Music.start` now is a no-op; intro-dismiss click bubbles through capture listener first so the bubble-phase `start()` sees `gestureReady=true`.
- **PWA first-install reload skip** (index.html): `wasControlled = !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller` captured at script-load time. `handleUpdate` returns early when `!wasControlled` — that's a first-install (no prior SW), and reloading is a wasted bounce that visibly flashed phrase-A → phrase-B on every Edge browser-restart with the user's "clear cache on close" setting (cache cleared, SW persists, page loads OLD intro.js from SW cache, new SW installs in background, posts SW_UPDATED → old code triggered reload, new code paints different phrase). Real update flow (existing SW + new APP_VERSION via /release) still reloads because `wasControlled` is true.
- **game.js → Maze state pollution between PotD and Marathon** (game.js startPuzzle callback): the diff check used to gate `Maze.setQuadMode/setPathCount` behind game.js's *cached* locals. PotD mutates Maze directly without routing through this callback, so PotD-quad → Marathon-singular (with prev Marathon ALSO singular) left Maze.quadMode=true and crashed (rotate's quad branch dereferenced null `quadScramble[qr][qc]`; solutionEdges's un-scramble dance ran a 6×6 scramble against an 8×8 grid → `grid[qr0][qc0]` undefined). Fix: always sync Maze from `opts` (cheap assignment); only invalidate pre-gen when game.js's view diffs (worker cache is independent of Maze); Render.refit() when `Maze.quadMode`/`Maze.pathCount` actually changed (compared against the live state, not cached locals). Any future PotD-like module that mutates Maze directly will get correct Marathon state on the next callback.
- **Hints tracking + tiebreaker** (marathon.js, leaderboard, i18n, server schema): new `hintsUsed` counter, reset in `startGame`, bumped in `onHintUsed`. Submitted via `buildPayload` as `hintsUsed`. Server sort is `solved DESC, hints_used ASC, total_ms ASC, played_at ASC` — hints break ties BEFORE time (user's deliberate call: hints-heavy runs pay twice — once via the in-game timer penalty during play, once via the leaderboard sort). Leaderboard row shows the new column via `marathon.lbHints` + `marathon.lbSolved` keys (compact row form, added to all 15 langs; distinct from sentence-form `marathon.solvedCount` used by the game-over screen). PotD rows pad with empty `<span>`s so they align in the shared 6-col grid. Local-cache sort comparator mirrors server (handles entries-without-hintsUsed as 0).
- **PotD pre-warm + parallel start, banner suppression**: `PotD.init` fires `fetchTodaysPuzzles()` into `serverFetchPromise` on page load so the today-fetch is usually settled by the time the player picks a slot. `startPuzzle` no longer awaits today then start sequentially — `Promise.all([ensurePuzzleAvailable(slot), postStart(date, slot)])` parallelizes them (postStart only needs `date`, computed up front via `todayUTC()`). Added `serverFetchResolved` flag (set true in `.finally()` of the fetch promise) so the "Loading today's puzzles…" banner skips when the fetch already settled — repeat clicks after pre-warm now jump straight to "Generating…" if the slot still needs local gen.
- **PotD name entry + shared iOS PWA keyboard**: PotD's solve modal now mirrors marathon's name-entry pattern. `showSolveModal` takes `awaitName` flag — `true` shows input+Save, `false` is the original tap-to-dismiss for ineligible/offline runs. `marathon.setupMobileKeyboard` / `teardownMobileKeyboard` refactored to take `(input, onEnter)` params + module-level `mobileKeyboardOwnerInput` for restore tracking; exposed on Marathon's public API; PotD calls through. Defensive teardown in PotD's `quitToMenu` / `quitToLeaderboard` in case HUD Quit fires mid-name-entry.
- **Quad twin lock visual fix** (maze.js `togglePlayerLock`): quad branch was setting `_playerLocked` on just the clicked quad's 4 sub-tiles and returning. Now mirrors rotate's and applyHintAt's twin handling — looks up `grid[qr0][qc0]._twin.partner`, locks/unlocks both quads' 4 sub-tiles each. The rotate() block already refused to rotate twin pairs unless both were unlocked, so the lock semantics were correct — only the visual indicator was missing on the partner quad.
- **Continuous music across multi-puzzle replay** (music.js `startScriptedPlayback`): index-0 ("anchor") event of each recording's musicEvents now skips `playSong` when the named song is already playing. Without it, every cross-puzzle handoff restarted the same track from t=0 because each puzzle's recording starts with a `{t:0, songId:X}` anchor. Subsequent events (i > 0) are deliberate song changes the original player triggered, so those still fire unconditionally on their setTimeout schedule.
- **PotD checkmark refresh after solve** (potd.js): `setSlotState` now calls `refreshMenuIndicators()` at the end. Used to only fire from `init` and `ModePicker.onChange === 'potd'`, so a player who solved a PotD slot and went straight to the leaderboard didn't see the slot checkmark until they bounced to Marathon and back. Folding the refresh into the write means every state change drives a re-paint — both 'started' and 'solved' callers get the refresh for free.
- **'Anonymous' default replaces 'Player'**: marathon Save and PotD post-solve both submit `name || 'Anonymous'`. Player saw 'Player' as filler and asked for 'Anonymous'. New PotD dialog ALSO lets a player click Save on empty input to deliberately submit as 'Anonymous' — that's now the intended path for "I don't want to type a name." Existing 'Player' rows in DB are stale; user said they'd wipe before release.
- **Game-over "Back to menu" hidden when name row visible**: top-N save path is now just Save (empty input → Anonymous). User preference: fewer redundant exit paths. Defensive `gameOverMenu.hidden = false` default at top of `renderGameOver` covers zero-score / below-cap / fetch-failure branches that don't reach the explicit hide.
- **MIN_DIM_QUAD 6→4 (q1-3), MIN_DIM_QUAD_4PATH 8→6 (q4)**: quad Marathon starts smaller. `STARTER_PLAN` in game.js derives at runtime from `MARATHON.minDimFor`, so pre-gen worker picks up new starter sizes automatically. q4 at 6×6 logical (12×12 physical) is tighter than 8×8 was for the 4-path generator — flagged for revert if the worker starts visibly hanging on q4 starts.
- **Audio error retry cap** (music.js): `MAX_CONSECUTIVE_ERRORS = 4` + `consecutiveErrors` counter. The audio element's 'error' listener used to call `advanceToNext` unconditionally — with a 1-song menu pool and a transiently-broken URL, that infinite-looped + flooded the console with `audio error / play rejected` pairs. Counter resets on the 'playing' event (real success). Also: skip spurious "no src" errors (`audio.src` empty or === document.href) — those fire after `removeAttribute('src')+load()` in `stop()` and would burn the retry budget on a non-problem.
- **Edge cache-clear-on-shutdown gotcha**: Edge's "Cached images and files" option does NOT clear Service Worker registrations or Cache Storage. To force a clean state on browser restart, also need "Cookies and other site data" enabled at `edge://settings/clearBrowsingDataOnClose`, OR use DevTools → Application → Storage → "Clear site data", OR paste: `(async () => { for (const r of await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations()) await r.unregister(); for (const k of await caches.keys()) await caches.delete(k); location.reload(); })()` in the console. Bit me when debugging the "phrase A → phrase B" flash — the dev server was serving fresh code but Edge's persisted SW was still answering with the cached old `intro.js`.

## 2026-05-23 — Music replay + leaderboard tabs + build supersession

- **Music recording/replay**: each `Game.recording` now carries `musicEvents: [{t, songId}, ...]`. Captured via new `Music.addSongChangeListener` (multi-subscriber list, separate from legacy single-slot `onSongChange` still owned by the now-playing UI). Pre-first-move song changes OVERWRITE the t=0 entry (so "starting song" is whatever's actually playing the instant the player begins); post-first-move APPEND. Replayed by new `Music.startScriptedPlayback(events)` — sorts by t, schedules each via `setTimeout`, owns the timeline (`advanceToNext`/`skipNext`/`skipPrev` short-circuit while active). `playOneRecording` wraps in try/finally so cancel/error still calls `stopScriptedPlayback`. PotD sends `musicEvents` as a SEPARATE top-level submit field (vs Marathon where it rides inside the existing `events` full-recording JSON) — requires back-end change for PotD only.
- **Abandoned-build stomp fix** (game.js newPuzzle): replaced `if (isBuilding) return;` early-drop with seq-token supersession. Each call captures `const mySeq = ++newPuzzleSeq;`, checks `if (mySeq !== newPuzzleSeq) return;` after every await (and inside the 500ms bannerTimer self-callback). `waitForPreGen(rows, cols, mySeq)` takes the seq too so a worker-preempted build's poll self-cancels instead of leaking. Main-thread `Maze.init()` path has an unfixable race (mutates Maze in-place during await) — documented, no real-world impact since worker is always available on deploy.
- **Title-O quadMode pollution**: `Maze.quadMode` is NOT part of `snapshotState/loadSnapshot`. After playing a quad puzzle, title-renderer's 2×2 elbow snapshot was rendering as ONE quad tile (no segmentation). Fix in `title-renderer.js`: save `Maze.quadMode` → `setQuadMode(false)` → render → restore. Keep this in mind when adding any other module-level Maze flags — they need similar save/restore.
- **Leaderboard UI**: dropdown (8 options) → two stacked tab rows (`#leaderboardTileTabs` Singular/Quad + `#leaderboardPathTabs` 1-4). Shared `.lbSubTab` class so visual + hover scoping live in one place. Helpers `getActiveBoardType()` / `setActiveBoardType(type)` compose/decompose the slot identifier — every consumer that used to read/write `leaderboardSelect.value` goes through these.
- **Building-banner stuck bugs (2 fixed)**: (1) `game.js`'s quit callback referenced `banner` (which is `winBanner`!) when meaning `buildingBanner` — quit mid-build never hid the building banner. (2) Cache-hit `newPuzzle` didn't clear the banner up front, so a stale banner from a prior abandoned build painted over a fresh cache-hit game. Both fixed in `game.js`.
- **Menu music**: new `MUSIC_MENU_LIST_NAME = 'menu_only'` pool in config.js. Music.js: parallel `menuPlaylist` + own shuffle bag, `inMenuPhase` flag, `pickNext` routes to menu pool when phase is menu AND pool non-empty (falls through to intro/shuffle for projects without a menu list). Cache schema bumped `_v4` → `_v5`. `Music.setMenuPhase(bool)` exported. Intro dismiss sets menu phase; marathon/PotD start sets game phase; goToMenu/quitToMenu restart with fresh menu song — gated by `Music.isMenuSongPlaying()` so leaderboard close doesn't interrupt menu music that's already playing.
- **iOS PWA on-screen keyboard** for `#gameOverName` input (mirrors Tantro's pattern). Triggered only when `window.navigator.standalone` is true. setupMobileKeyboard / teardownMobileKeyboard in marathon.js; CSS `#customKeyboard` in styles.css with `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` for the home-indicator gesture bar. NOT enabled on non-iOS-PWA — native keyboard is fine elsewhere.
- **Opening intro/warning screen** (`intro.js` + `warnings.txt` + `#introOverlay` markup). Ridiculousness-style WARNING + random silly activity from warnings.txt (user curates that file; bland fallback list in intro.js if fetch fails). Music + Full Screen toggles; F11/PageUp/PageDown global key-toggle for fullscreen (Tantro's exact set). Suppressed by `?nointro=true` and in debug mode.
- **PotD strict path-count**: maze.js's 3+ paths AND 2-path AND quad branches now loop indefinitely until `bestCount >= target` (never accept fewer paths than requested). Drop `hurryFlag`'s `target-1` shortcut — letting hurry accept fewer paths violated the selected-count contract. Paired with `MARATHON.minDimFor(quadMode, pathCount)` helper that bumps 4-path puzzles to `MIN_DIM_SINGULAR_4PATH=10` / `MIN_DIM_QUAD_4PATH=8` so the strict loop converges fast.
- **Defaults changed**: `HINT_PENALTY_FRACTION` 0.33→0.25 (live), `DEF_TILE_FACE` 0.80→0.69, `DEF_PATH_ALPHA` 0.14→0.11. Opacity defaults only hit fresh installs (loadFloat keeps existing localStorage values). Music defaults `0.88` / `0.42`.
- **Gate click buffer**: `gates.js HIT_BUFFER_FRAC` 0.20 → 0.07. Was eating taps adjacent to gates and rotating gates instead of tiles.
- **Credit-link gotcha**: anchors must have `data-credit-link="<id>"` attribute for share.js's capture-phase delegate to record the click. NEW tags added this session: `suno-dog` (creditsSuno6), `aspca` (donation link). Untagged anchors silently don't count — this has bitten us once already.
- **Hover styles** on `.menuModeBtn`, `#menuLeaderboardBtn`, `.menu-dropdown-btn`, `.lbSubTab` are scoped to `@media (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)` so iOS Safari doesn't eat the first tap as a "hover". Apply the same scoping when adding any new tappable element with a `:hover` style.

## 2026-05-23 — Visit tracking + share UIs + PotD loading banner

- **`tracking.js` module** exposes `recordVisit`/`recordStart`/`recordFinish`/`recordShare`/`recordCreditsClick`. All suppressed at the module layer when `DevMode.isActive()` is true — dev runs don't pollute aggregate stats. Each fetch wrapped in a 5s AbortController; fire-and-forget (no surfaced errors). `visitId` lives in module scope; on bot UAs the server returns -1 and all subsequent PATCH calls become no-ops. Loaded LAST in the script chain so DevMode and Potd/Marathon are guaranteed available before tracking calls fire.
- **`share.js` module** owns BOTH share surfaces via `[data-share-platform]` delegation: the minimalist `.shareIcon` row inside `#gameOver` + `#potdSolveTransition`, and the larger `#shareOverlay` "Enjoying the game?" popup. Popup fires after the **2nd cumulative finish** (Marathon `gameOver` + PotD `onSolve` both count) — NOT after 2nd start, which is the obvious mistake. Persisted counters: `<slug>_share_finish_count`, `<slug>_share_dismissed`. Console re-arm: `Share.resetDismiss()`.
- **Share URL defaults to `window.location.origin + window.location.pathname`** — works on any host (Netlify, itch.io iframe, custom domain). If you ever want a canonical short URL (e.g., `digeratist.itch.io/circuitousness` rather than the iframe origin), edit `getShareUrl()` in `share.js`.
- **Credits-link tracking** via `[data-credit-link="..."]` data attribute + capture-phase delegate in share.js. Currently tagged in index.html: `suno-digeratist`, `official-intelligence`, `imaginary-friend`. **New credits anchors must include the data attribute to be tracked** — without it the click silently doesn't count.
- **i18n**: 5 new keys (`share.title`, `share.subtitle`, `share.copyLink`, `share.dontShowAgain`, `share.text`) across all 15 languages. `share.text` concatenates `PROJECT_NAME` so rename propagates automatically.
- **PotD "Loading today's puzzle…" banner** in `startPuzzle`, shown immediately after canvas clear. Fixes the dead-screen regression introduced by yesterday's localStorage puzzle cache: when the snapshot was in cache, `ensurePuzzleAvailable` returned instantly without showing a banner, leaving the player staring at a blank background for several seconds while `postStart` awaited the session-token round-trip. Banner persists through the postStart wait and is hidden before the ineligible-retry disclaimer modal so they don't overlap.

## 2026-05-23 — iOS audio, PotD reliability, leaderboard wipe button

- **iOS audio support** mirrors TANTЯO's pattern: `IS_IOS_AUDIO` flag in `config.js` (iPad/iPhone UA, or MacIntel+touch for iPadOS-as-Mac); `MUSIC_BASE_URL` swaps to `AppConfig.GAME_API + '/music/Music/'` on iOS; SFX **always** uses the back-end proxy (CORS for `fetch+decodeAudioData` even on desktop, not just iOS). Sfx fully rewritten on Web Audio API — `BufferSource` + `GainNode` per shot, `linearRampToValueAtTime` for fades, `source.loop=true` for loops. AudioContext auto-resumes on first click/touchend/keydown so iOS unblocks. Music keeps its single `<audio>` element but on iOS only, captures the first user gesture and runs a silent `audio.play()` to "bless" the element so later async-context `.play()` calls succeed. **Requires back-end deploy** for SFX to work anywhere (not just iOS) — pure on-disk update of front-end will give 404s on all SFX URLs until the music_proxy endpoint lands on Render.
- **PotD fetch race fix**: shared `serverFetchPromise` between `devSeedAllSlots` (dev-mode boot kick) and `ensurePuzzleAvailable` (player click). Old `serverFetchAttempted` boolean caused player click during boot fetch to see attempted=true, skip the load step entirely, show "Generating…" while actually waiting on the server, and queue a redundant generation if it ever resolved. Shared promise = single in-flight fetch, correct "Loading…" banner, no spurious generation.
- **PotD fetch 8s timeout** via `AbortController` in `fetchTodaysPuzzles`. Old code awaited the fetch with no cap — if Render hung, the player was stuck on "Loading today's puzzles…" forever. Now after 8s the abort kills the fetch, the function returns null, and `ensurePuzzleAvailable` falls through to local generation. Console gets `PotD: /today fetch failed timeout after 8000ms`.
- **localStorage cache for today's PotD snapshots** (`<slug>_potd_puzzles_<date>`): `loadPuzzlesCache`/`savePuzzlesCache`/`pruneOldPuzzleCaches` in potd.js. Snapshots are immutable per (date,slot) so caching is always safe. First click after hard refresh now hits localStorage (instant) instead of paying ~1-3s network round-trip on paid Render. Background server fetch still fires to merge in any newly-seeded slots. **Trapdoor**: if snapshot shape ever changes server-side without bumping a cache version, returning players' first load could deserialize a stale shape — add a `version` field to `savePuzzlesCache` payload if that risk materializes.
- **PotD worker resilience** (potd.js): MAX_WORKER_FAILURES=3 counter, per-error `potdWorker = null` (recreate next call), `potdWorkerFailures = 0` on successful response. Old single-strike behavior permanently disabled the worker on a single transient error, forcing main-thread (UI-freezing) generation for the rest of the session. Also extracted ErrorEvent fields (`message`/`filename`/`lineno`) in `onerror` and the catch in `runQueue` — the old `Logger.warn('PotD gen failed', slot, e)` logged `e` as `"Event"` (its toString), useless for diagnosis.
- **`isDebugMode` runtime-check fix** in game.js: was a boot-time snapshot used for both 'N' key + win-banner click handlers. With `?dev=true` Ctrl+D making the mode mutable, a session that started in game-mode and Ctrl+D'd to debug had no advance affordance after solving — the handlers were never registered. Switched to a `inDebugMode()` runtime check that reads the current `<html>` class. Same fix-pattern applies anywhere else `isDebugMode` gates registration of toggle-able UI; only one place needed it but worth knowing.
- **Wipe Leaderboards debug button** (`#wipeLeaderboardsBtn`): POSTs `/api/admin/wipe-leaderboards`, then `Marathon.wipeLocalLeaderboards()` (new helper) drops every `_lb_*`/`_potd_lb_*`/`_own_rec_*`/`_lastPlayerName`/`_lb_pending` localStorage key. Status line reports server counts + local key count. **Existing "Reset PotD (today)" button** stays untouched (it wipes today's PUZZLES + sessions; the new button wipes ALL SCORES across all dates — different scope, both useful). Both buttons + handler + Marathon helper + back-end endpoint are all tagged with "Remove before public release" comments so cleanup is grep-able.

## 2026-05-22 — Visual settings, twin density tuning, dev mode

- **Twin density doubled**: `twinPairCount` now uses `gridScale() * 2` (8×8 → 11 pairs vs 3 before); `quadTwinPairCount` uses `(ROWS + COLS) / 2` instead of `/4`. **Critical**: the `*2` is INSIDE the twin formula, NOT in `gridScale()` itself — `gridScale()` is also consumed by `minPathLength` (×2.25) and `minShortcutTwists` (×1.25), so doubling at the source would have rippled into path-length and shortcut floors and changed puzzle character. User wants to tune density independently of difficulty floors. If twin density feels wrong, change the `* 2` to `* 1.5` or drop it; the path/shortcut floors stay put either way.
- **TWIN_COLORS expanded 6→16** (maze.js): existing palette only had 6 pastels, so the increased pair count hit visible color repeats. Added yellow/indigo/mint/light-brown/sky/lime/rose/tan/sage/mauve. Red intentionally absent (reserved for hint-locked tiles). Past 16 pairs they cycle modulo 16; in practice players rarely hit that.
- **Visual settings popup**: 6 player-tunable knobs lifted from the dev-only debug panel — Tile color, Tile opacity, Path color, Path opacity, Path width, Bevel size. Slider domains preserved from debug panel (path width 5..45 → 0.05..0.45; bevel 1..20 → 0.01..0.20). **Default tile face opacity bumped 0.74 → 0.80** in render.js — update both render.js AND the debug-panel slider's `value` attribute in index.html if changing again, they have to stay in sync. All persist via `Settings` slug-namespaced localStorage; `Render.set*` called live on `input` event so canvas updates as the player drags. "Reset visuals to defaults" link styled as subtle text-link, right-aligned, calls `Settings.resetVisualsToDefaults()`. Labels use new `.settingsVisualLabel` class (font-size matches `.settingsRow label`, min-width 7rem) so all 6 visual rows align vertically and read at the same weight regardless of `<label>` vs `<span>` tag.
- **Debug panel max-width**: capped at 20rem; `#preGenStatus` cache list (space-separated entries) wraps naturally instead of pushing the panel across the canvas. With 8 starters + 3 lookahead = 11 entries, the unbounded version was >1000px wide.
- **`?dev=true` mode** (new `dev-mode.js`): gates three capabilities. (1) **Ctrl+D** toggles `<html>` between `mode-game`/`mode-debug` — initial mode still set by `?debug=true`, dev=true only enables the LIVE toggle. Skips when focus is on an input/textarea so name entry isn't interrupted. (2) **Daily PotD watcher** polls every minute for UTC date change → calls new `Potd.devSeedAllSlots()` (silent variant of `ensurePuzzleAvailable` — fetches server-seeded, queues missing, awaits drain). Also kicks once at boot (2s delay so it doesn't fight the rest of init). (3) `DevMode.isActive()` exposed for future admin-stat code to suppress tracking from dev sessions — the analytics code isn't built yet but the hook is in place.
- **`?dev=true` and `?debug=true` are orthogonal**: `?dev=true` alone → starts in game mode, Ctrl+D enters debug. `?dev=true&debug=true` → starts in debug, Ctrl+D returns to game. `?debug=true` alone (no dev) → starts in debug, Ctrl+D does nothing.

## 2026-05-22 — End credits + always-warm starter pre-gen

- **End-credits sequence** mirrors TANTЯO: `credits.js` runs a rAF scroll loop behind a translucent `.credits-overlay`; body gets `credits-rolling` class which the CSS uses to reposition `#gameOver` (top:6vh) and `#potdSolveTransition` (top:22%) into the upper third with z-index 1001. Triggered from `Marathon.gameOver()` and `Potd.onSolve()`; torn down in `goToMenu`/`quitToMenu`/`showLeaderboard`. **First 4s of credits ignore click-to-pause** (`PAUSE_GRACE_MS` in credits.js) so stray clicks carried over from dismissing the game-end popup don't instantly freeze the scroll — user explicitly hit this.
- **Music modes** replaced single `musicMuted` boolean with `musicMode` enum (`none|game_playlist|credits|game_plus_credits`) persisted under `_setting_musicMode`. `priorMode` (separate localStorage key) remembers the last non-`none` choice so the mute toggle restores it on unmute instead of silently reverting to `game_playlist`.
